==> .github/workflows/benchmark_ci.yml <==
name: Validation & Universal Benchmark Suite (mshqc vs Psi4)

on:
  push:
    branches: [ "" ]

  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  universal_benchmark:
    name: Run Pytest & Benchmark Suite (mshqc Wheel vs Psi4)
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    env:
      OMP_NUM_THREADS: "4"
      TBLIS_NUM_THREADS: "4"
      MKL_NUM_THREADS: "1"
      OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS: "1"
      HDF5_USE_FILE_LOCKING: "FALSE"
      PYTHONUNBUFFERED: "1"

    steps:
      - name: Checkout Repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python 3.12 (for precompiled mshqc PyPI wheel)
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.12"

      - name: Install Precompiled mshqc Wheel from PyPI (No C++ Recompile)
        run: |
          python3 -m venv /tmp/mshqc_venv
          /tmp/mshqc_venv/bin/pip install --upgrade pip
          /tmp/mshqc_venv/bin/pip uninstall -y mshqc || true
          /tmp/mshqc_venv/bin/pip install mshqc --no-cache-dir pytest

      # --- 1. RUN PYTEST UNTUK MSHQC (Simpan log tampilan ke .txt) ---
      - name: Run Automated Pytest Validation Suite (test_mshqc.py)
        shell: bash -eo pipefail {0}
        run: |
          /tmp/mshqc_venv/bin/pytest tests/test_mshqc.py -v -s 2>&1 | tee 01_pytest_mshqc_log.txt

      # --- 2. JALANKAN TEST MSHQC2 (Tampil real-time & simpan ke .txt) ---
      - name: Run mshqc Benchmark (test_mshqc2.py)
        shell: bash -eo pipefail {0}
        run: |
          /tmp/mshqc_venv/bin/python -u tests/test_mshqc2.py 2>&1 | tee 02_mshqc_out.txt
          cp 02_mshqc_out.txt mshqc_out.txt

      # --- 3. SET UP PSI4 ---
      - name: Set up Micromamba & Install Psi4 (conda-forge)
        uses: mamba-org/setup-micromamba@v2
        with:
          environment-name: psi4_env
          create-args: >-
            -c conda-forge
            python=3.11
            psi4
            numpy
            scipy

      # --- 4. JALANKAN BENC.PY PSI4 (Tampil real-time & simpan ke .txt) ---
      - name: Run Psi4 Benchmark (benc.py)
        shell: bash -el {0}
        run: |
          set -o pipefail
          micromamba activate psi4_env
          python -u tests/benc.py 2>&1 | tee 03_psi4_out.txt
          cp 03_psi4_out.txt psi4_out.txt

      # --- 5. BANDINGKAN HASIL (TEST_MSHQC2 vs BENC.PY) ---
      - name: Compare Head-to-Head mshqc vs Psi4
        shell: bash {0}
        run: |
          /tmp/mshqc_venv/bin/python -u tests/compare_mshqc_psi4.py mshqc_out.txt psi4_out.txt 2>&1 | tee 04_compare_mshqc_psi4_log.txt || true

      # --- 6. UJI SKRIPSI BAB 4 ---
      - name: Run mshqc Bab 4 Benchmark
        shell: bash -eo pipefail {0}
        run: |
          /tmp/mshqc_venv/bin/python -u tests/run_mshqc_bab4.py
          cp mshqc_bab4_test.txt 05_mshqc_bab4_running_log.txt

      - name: Run Psi4 Bab 4 Benchmark
        shell: bash -el {0}
        run: |
          set -o pipefail
          micromamba activate psi4_env
          python -u tests/run_psi4_bab4.py
          cp psi4_bab4_test.txt 06_psi4_bab4_running_log.txt

      - name: Compare Bab 4 Results
        shell: bash {0}
        run: |
          /tmp/mshqc_venv/bin/python -u tests/compare_bab4.py || true
          cp test_bab4_benchmark.txt 07_compare_bab4_log.txt

      # --- 7. RUNNER UNIVERSAL BAWAAN ---
      - name: Run mshqc Universal Benchmark
        shell: bash -eo pipefail {0}
        run: |
          /tmp/mshqc_venv/bin/python -u tests/run_mshqc_universal.py 2>&1 | tee 08_mshqc_universal_running_log.txt

      - name: Run Psi4 Universal Reference Benchmark
        shell: bash -el {0}
        run: |
          set -o pipefail
          micromamba activate psi4_env
          python -u tests/run_psi4_universal.py 2>&1 | tee 09_psi4_universal_running_log.txt

      - name: Generate Universal Comparison Table
        shell: bash {0}
        run: |
          /tmp/mshqc_venv/bin/python -u tests/compare_universal.py || true
          cp test_universal_validation.txt 10_compare_universal_log.txt

      # --- 8. UPLOAD SEMUA LOG DALAM FORMAT .TXT MURNI ---
      - name: Upload All Running Logs (.txt only)
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: mshqc-vs-psi4-benchmark-logs-txt
          path: |
            01_pytest_mshqc_log.txt
            02_mshqc_out.txt
            03_psi4_out.txt
            04_compare_mshqc_psi4_log.txt
            05_mshqc_bab4_running_log.txt
            06_psi4_bab4_running_log.txt
            07_compare_bab4_log.txt
            08_mshqc_universal_running_log.txt
            09_psi4_universal_running_log.txt
            10_compare_universal_log.txt
==> .github/workflows/publish_now.yml <==
name: Instant Publish Artifacts to PyPI and GitHub Release

on:
  push:
    branches: [""]
    tags: [""]
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      run_id:
        description: "GitHub Actions Run ID yang memiliki artifact wheel"
        required: false
        default: "43217985325"

permissions:
  contents: write
  actions: read

jobs:
  publish_existing_wheels:
    name: Grab Artifacts, Inject README & Publish to PyPI
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - name: Checkout Repository (ambil README.md & pyproject.toml terbaru)
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.12"

      - name: Extract Version from pyproject.toml (Tanpa awalan v)
        id: meta
        run: |
          VERSION=$(python3 -c "import tomllib; print(tomllib.load(open('pyproject.toml', 'rb'))['project']['version'])")
          echo "version=$VERSION" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          echo "Target Release Version: $VERSION"

      - name: Download Compiled Wheels & sdist from Run
        uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
        with:
          run-id: ${{ inputs.run_id || '43217985325' }}
          github-token: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
          pattern: cibw-*
          path: dist
          merge-multiple: true

      - name: Inject GitHub README.md into All Wheels (Fix Windows CRLF & macOS MKL)
        env:
          TARGET_VER: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.version }}
        run: |
          python3 -m pip install --upgrade pip build twine
          python3 - << 'EOF'
          import base64, glob, hashlib, os, re, shutil, subprocess, sys, zipfile
          from pathlib import Path

          target_ver = os.environ["TARGET_VER"].strip()
          readme_text = Path("README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8").replace("\r\n", "\n").strip()

          def b64_nopad(digest: bytes) -> str:
              return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(digest).decode("ascii").rstrip("=")

          for whl_str in sorted(glob.glob("dist/*.whl")):
              whl_path = Path(whl_str)
              new_whl_name = re.sub(r"^mshqc-[^-]+-", f"mshqc-{target_ver}-", whl_path.name)
              new_whl_path = whl_path.parent / new_whl_name
              tmp_whl = whl_path.with_suffix(".whl.tmp")
              new_dist_info = f"mshqc-{target_ver}.dist-info"
              records = []

              with zipfile.ZipFile(whl_path, "r") as zin, \
                   zipfile.ZipFile(tmp_whl, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:

                  for item in zin.infolist():
                      fname = item.filename
                      if fname.endswith(".dist-info/RECORD"):
                          continue

                      data = zin.read(fname)
                      if ".dist-info/" in fname:
                          suffix = fname.split(".dist-info/", 1)[1]
                          fname = f"{new_dist_info}/{suffix}"

                      if fname == "mshqc/__init__.py":
                          txt = data.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").replace("\r\n", "\n")
                          txt = re.sub(r'__version__\s*=\s*"[^"]+"', f'__version__ = "{target_ver}"', txt)
                          data = txt.encode("utf-8")

                      if fname.endswith(".dist-info/METADATA"):
                          # Normalisasi CRLF (\r\n) dari Windows ke LF (\n) sebelum split!
                          raw_meta = data.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n")
                          headers_block = raw_meta.split("\n\n", 1)[0].strip()

                          clean_headers = []
                          for line in headers_block.splitlines():
                              if line.startswith("Version:"):
                                  clean_headers.append(f"Version: {target_ver}")
                              elif line.startswith("Requires-Dist:") and "mkl" in line:
                                  clean_headers.append('Requires-Dist: mkl ; sys_platform != "darwin"')
                              elif line.startswith("Description-Content-Type:"):
                                  continue
                              else:
                                  clean_headers.append(line)

                          clean_headers.append("Description-Content-Type: text/markdown")
                          if not any("github.com/syahrulhidayat/mshqc" in h for h in clean_headers):
                              clean_headers.append("Project-URL: Homepage, https://github.com/syahrulhidayat/mshqc")
                              clean_headers.append("Project-URL: Repository, https://github.com/syahrulhidayat/mshqc")

                          data = ("\n".join(clean_headers) + "\n\n" + readme_text + "\n").encode("utf-8")

                      info_copy = zipfile.ZipInfo(filename=fname, date_time=item.date_time)
                      info_copy.compress_type = zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED
                      info_copy.external_attr = item.external_attr
                      zout.writestr(info_copy, data)

                      sha = b64_nopad(hashlib.sha256(data).digest())
                      records.append(f"{fname},sha256={sha},{len(data)}")

                  rec_fname = f"{new_dist_info}/RECORD"
                  records.append(f"{rec_fname},,")
                  zout.writestr(rec_fname, ("\n".join(records) + "\n").encode("utf-8"))

              whl_path.unlink()
              tmp_whl.replace(new_whl_path)
              print(f"[OK] Synced & Repacked: {new_whl_path.name}")

          # Bangun ulang sdist (.tar.gz) agar versinya persis sama dengan pyproject.toml
          for old_tar in glob.glob("dist/*.tar.gz"):
              os.remove(old_tar)
          subprocess.run(["git", "clone", "--depth", "1", "https://github.com/syahrulhidayat/mshqc-basis.git", "/tmp/mshqc-basis"], check=True)
          Path("data/basis").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
          for gbs in Path("/tmp/mshqc-basis/data/basis").glob("*.gbs"):
              shutil.copy2(gbs, Path("data/basis") / gbs.name)
          shutil.rmtree("/tmp/mshqc-basis", ignore_errors=True)
          subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "build", "--sdist", "--outdir", "dist"], check=True)
          EOF
          python3 -m twine check dist/*
          ls -lh dist/

      - name: Publish Wheels & sdist to PyPI
        uses: pypa/gh-action-pypi-publish@release/v1
        with:
          password: ${{ secrets.PYPI_API_TOKEN }}
          skip-existing: true

      - name: Create GitHub Release (Tanpa awalan v)
        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v2
        with:
          tag_name: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.version }}
          name: "mshqc ${{ steps.meta.outputs.version }}"
          body_path: README.md
          files: |
            dist/*.whl
            dist/*.tar.gz
          generate_release_notes: true
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
==> .github/workflows/release_public.yml <==
name: Compile and Release Wheel to Public Repo

on:
  push:
    branches: ["build-pypi"]
    tags: ["*"]
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  build_wheels:
    name: Build wheels on ${{ matrix.os }}
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        os: [ubuntu-latest, macos-latest, windows-latest]

    steps:
      - name: Checkout Source Code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          fetch-depth: 1

      - name: Fetch Basis Sets from mshqc-basis
        shell: bash
        run: |
          git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/syahrulhidayat/mshqc-basis.git /tmp/mshqc-basis
          mkdir -p data/basis
          cp -rf /tmp/mshqc-basis/data/basis/* data/basis/
          rm -rf /tmp/mshqc-basis

      - name: Set up Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.12"

      - name: Cache Static Dependencies (Linux)
        if: runner.os == 'Linux'
        uses: actions/cache@v4
        with:
          path: .cache_deps
          key: manylinux_2_28-static-deps-v6.1.2-tblis-v1.2.0

      # ========================================================================
      # STEP KHUSUS MACOS: Cache + Kompilasi libcint & TBLIS di Host macOS
      # ========================================================================
      - name: Restore Cached C/C++ Dependencies (macOS)
        if: runner.os == 'macOS'
        id: cache-macos-deps
        uses: actions/cache/restore@v4
        with:
          path: .cache_deps_macos
          key: macos-arm64-deps-v6.1.2-tblis-v1.2.0-v1

      - name: Build C/C++ Dependencies (macOS)
        if: runner.os == 'macOS'
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -e
          brew install --quiet cmake ninja eigen libomp hdf5 openblas lapack || true
          sudo mkdir -p /usr/local/include /usr/local/lib

          if [ -f ".cache_deps_macos/lib/libtblis.a" ] && [ -f ".cache_deps_macos/lib/libcint.a" ]; then
            echo "Restoring cached libcint and TBLIS to /usr/local..."
            sudo cp -rf .cache_deps_macos/include/* /usr/local/include/
            sudo cp -rf .cache_deps_macos/lib/* /usr/local/lib/
          else
            git clone --depth 1 -b v6.1.2 https://github.com/sunqm/libcint.git /tmp/libcint
            cmake -S /tmp/libcint -B /tmp/libcint/build \
                  -DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX=/usr/local \
                  -DCMAKE_PREFIX_PATH="/opt/homebrew;/opt/homebrew/opt/openblas;/opt/homebrew/opt/lapack" \
                  -DCMAKE_POSITION_INDEPENDENT_CODE=ON \
                  -DCMAKE_C_FLAGS="-O3 -fPIC -Wno-incompatible-pointer-types -Wno-implicit-function-declaration -Wno-deprecated-non-prototype" \
                  -DENABLE_EXAMPLE=0 -DENABLE_TEST=0 -DBUILD_SHARED_LIBS=OFF
            cmake --build /tmp/libcint/build -j$(sysctl -n hw.ncpu)
            sudo cmake --install /tmp/libcint/build

            git clone --depth 1 -b v1.2.0 https://github.com/devinamatthews/tblis.git /tmp/tblis
            cd /tmp/tblis
            git submodule update --init --recursive
            perl -pi -e 's/SIGSTKSZ/8192/g' src/external/catch/catch.hpp
            perl -pi -e 'print "#include <limits>\n" if $. == 1' src/external/marray/include/short_vector.hpp
            perl -pi -e 'print "#include <limits>\n" if $. == 1' src/external/marray/include/range.hpp
            perl -pi -e 'print "#include <limits>\n" if $. == 1' src/util/thread.h
            perl -pi -e 'print "#include <stdlib.h>\n" if $. == 1' src/external/tci/tci/task_set.c
            perl -pi -e 's|#include <x86intrin.h>|#if defined(__GNUC__) && (defined(__x86_64__) \|\| defined(__i386__))\n#include <x86intrin.h>\n#else\n#undef __AVX512F__\n#undef __AVX__\n#undef __SSE2__\n#endif|g' src/external/marray/include/expression.hpp

            perl -pi -e 's/enum \{VENDOR_ARM, VENDOR_UNKNOWN\}/enum {VENDOR_ARM, VENDOR_UNKNOWN};/g' src/util/cpuid.hpp
            perl -pi -e 's/enum \{MODEL_ARMV7, MODEL_ARMV8, MODEL_UNKNOWN\}/enum {MODEL_ARMV7, MODEL_ARMV8, MODEL_UNKNOWN};/g' src/util/cpuid.hpp
            perl -pi -e 's/strtoi\(ptno, pos, 16\)/((void)pos, (int)strtol(ptno.c_str(), NULL, 16))/g' src/util/cpuid.cxx

            ./configure --prefix=/usr/local --enable-static --disable-shared --with-pic --without-hwloc \
                        --enable-config=reference \
                        CFLAGS="-O3 -fPIC -Wno-implicit-function-declaration -Wno-incompatible-function-pointer-types" \
                        CXXFLAGS="-O3 -fPIC -std=c++17 -D_LIBCPP_ENABLE_CXX17_REMOVED_UNARY_BINARY_FUNCTION -D_LIBCPP_ENABLE_CXX17_REMOVED_RANDOM_SHUFFLE"
            make -j$(sysctl -n hw.ncpu) bin_PROGRAMS= noinst_PROGRAMS= check_PROGRAMS=
            sudo make install bin_PROGRAMS= noinst_PROGRAMS= check_PROGRAMS=

            cd "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE"
            mkdir -p .cache_deps_macos/include .cache_deps_macos/lib
            cp -rf /usr/local/include/cint*.h /usr/local/include/tci* /usr/local/include/tblis .cache_deps_macos/include/
            cp -rf /usr/local/lib/libcint.a /usr/local/lib/libtblis.a /usr/local/lib/libtci.a .cache_deps_macos/lib/
          fi

      - name: Save Cached C/C++ Dependencies (macOS)
        if: runner.os == 'macOS' && steps.cache-macos-deps.outputs.cache-hit != 'true'
        uses: actions/cache/save@v4
        with:
          path: .cache_deps_macos
          key: macos-arm64-deps-v6.1.2-tblis-v1.2.0-v1

      # ========================================================================
      # STEP KHUSUS WINDOWS: Instalasi Conda + Cache & Build libcint/TBLIS
      # ========================================================================
      - name: Install MSVC Libraries via Conda (Windows)
        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
        shell: cmd
        run: |
          call %CONDA%\condabin\conda.bat create -p C:\deps -c conda-forge hdf5 eigen mkl-devel -y

      - name: Restore Cached libcint & TBLIS (Windows)
        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
        id: cache-win-deps
        uses: actions/cache/restore@v4
        with:
          path: .cache_deps_windows
          key: windows-amd64-deps-v6.1.2-tblis-v1.2.0-v1

      - name: Build libcint & TBLIS via MSYS2 MinGW64 (Windows)
        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
        shell: C:\msys64\usr\bin\bash.exe --noprofile --norc -eo pipefail "{0}"
        env:
          MSYSTEM: MINGW64
          MSYS2_PATH_TYPE: inherit
        run: |
          export PATH="/mingw64/bin:/usr/bin:$PATH"
          mkdir -p /c/deps/Library/include /c/deps/Library/lib /c/deps/Library/bin

          if [ -f ".cache_deps_windows/lib/tblis.lib" ] && [ -f ".cache_deps_windows/lib/cint.lib" ]; then
            echo "Restoring cached libcint and TBLIS to C:/deps/Library..."
            cp -rf .cache_deps_windows/include/* /c/deps/Library/include/
            cp -rf .cache_deps_windows/lib/* /c/deps/Library/lib/
            cp -rf .cache_deps_windows/bin/* /c/deps/Library/bin/
          else
            if ! command -v make >/dev/null 2>&1 || ! command -v diff >/dev/null 2>&1; then
              for attempt in 1 2 3; do
                pacman -Sy --noconfirm --needed --disable-download-timeout make diffutils && break || sleep 5
              done
            fi
            if ! command -v /mingw64/bin/gcc.exe >/dev/null 2>&1; then
              for attempt in 1 2 3; do
                pacman -Sy --noconfirm --needed --disable-download-timeout \
                  mingw-w64-x86_64-gcc mingw-w64-x86_64-gcc-fortran mingw-w64-x86_64-libgomp \
                  mingw-w64-x86_64-libatomic mingw-w64-x86_64-make && break || sleep 5
              done
            fi

            CMAKE_EXE=$(command -v /mingw64/bin/cmake.exe || command -v cmake.exe || echo "/c/Program Files/CMake/bin/cmake.exe")

            cp -f /mingw64/include/pthread*.h /mingw64/include/sched.h /c/deps/Library/include/ 2>/dev/null || true

            cat << 'EOF' > /c/deps/Library/include/unistd.h
          #ifndef _UNISTD_H
          #define _UNISTD_H
          #include <io.h>
          #include <process.h>
          #include <BaseTsd.h>
          typedef SSIZE_T ssize_t;

          #if defined(_MSC_VER) && defined(__cplusplus)
          #include <intrin.h>
          #include <type_traits>
          #ifndef __ATOMIC_RELAXED
          #define __ATOMIC_RELAXED 0
          #define __ATOMIC_CONSUME 1
          #define __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE 2
          #define __ATOMIC_RELEASE 3
          #define __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL 4
          #define __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST 5
          #endif
          template <typename T>
          static inline T __mshqc_atomic_load_n(const volatile T* ptr, int) {
              T val = *ptr;
              _ReadWriteBarrier();
              return val;
          }
          template <typename T, typename U>
          static inline void __mshqc_atomic_store_n(volatile T* ptr, U val, int) {
              _ReadWriteBarrier();
              *ptr = static_cast<T>(val);
              _ReadWriteBarrier();
          }
          template <typename T, typename U>
          static inline T __mshqc_atomic_fetch_add(volatile T* ptr, U val, int) {
              if constexpr (sizeof(T) == 4) return (T)_InterlockedExchangeAdd((volatile long*)ptr, (long)val);
              else if constexpr (sizeof(T) == 8) return (T)_InterlockedExchangeAdd64((volatile __int64*)ptr, (__int64)val);
              else { T old = *ptr; *ptr += (T)val; return old; }
          }
          template <typename T, typename U>
          static inline T __mshqc_atomic_add_fetch(volatile T* ptr, U val, int m) {
              return __mshqc_atomic_fetch_add(ptr, val, m) + static_cast<T>(val);
          }
          template <typename T, typename U>
          static inline T __mshqc_atomic_sub_fetch(volatile T* ptr, U val, int m) {
              return __mshqc_atomic_fetch_add(ptr, -static_cast<std::make_signed_t<T>>(val), m) - static_cast<T>(val);
          }
          template <typename T, typename U>
          static inline T __mshqc_atomic_exchange_n(volatile T* ptr, U val, int) {
              if constexpr (sizeof(T) == 4) return (T)_InterlockedExchange((volatile long*)ptr, (long)val);
              else if constexpr (sizeof(T) == 8) return (T)_InterlockedExchange64((volatile __int64*)ptr, (__int64)val);
              else return (T)_InterlockedExchange8((volatile char*)ptr, (char)val);
          }
          template <typename T, typename U>
          static inline bool __mshqc_atomic_compare_exchange_n(volatile T* ptr, T* expected, U desired, bool, int, int) {
              if constexpr (sizeof(T) == 4) {
                  long exp = (long)*expected;
                  long prev = _InterlockedCompareExchange((volatile long*)ptr, (long)desired, exp);
                  if (prev == exp) return true;
                  *expected = (T)prev;
                  return false;
              } else if constexpr (sizeof(T) == 8) {
                  __int64 exp = (__int64)*expected;
                  __int64 prev = _InterlockedCompareExchange64((volatile __int64*)ptr, (__int64)desired, exp);
                  if (prev == exp) return true;
                  *expected = (T)prev;
                  return false;
              } else {
                  char exp = (char)*expected;
                  char prev = _InterlockedCompareExchange8((volatile char*)ptr, (char)desired, exp);
                  if (prev == exp) return true;
                  *expected = (T)prev;
                  return false;
              }
          }
          static inline bool __mshqc_atomic_test_and_set(volatile void* ptr, int) {
              return _InterlockedExchange8((volatile char*)ptr, 1) != 0;
          }
          static inline void __mshqc_atomic_clear(volatile void* ptr, int) {
              _ReadWriteBarrier();
              *(volatile char*)ptr = 0;
              _ReadWriteBarrier();
          }
          static inline void __mshqc_atomic_thread_fence(int) { _ReadWriteBarrier(); }
          static inline void __mshqc_atomic_signal_fence(int) { _ReadWriteBarrier(); }
          #define __atomic_load_n __mshqc_atomic_load_n
          #define __atomic_store_n __mshqc_atomic_store_n
          #define __atomic_fetch_add __mshqc_atomic_fetch_add
          #define __atomic_add_fetch __mshqc_atomic_add_fetch
          #define __atomic_sub_fetch __mshqc_atomic_sub_fetch
          #define __atomic_exchange_n __mshqc_atomic_exchange_n
          #define __atomic_compare_exchange_n __mshqc_atomic_compare_exchange_n
          #define __atomic_test_and_set __mshqc_atomic_test_and_set
          #define __atomic_clear __mshqc_atomic_clear
          #define __atomic_thread_fence __mshqc_atomic_thread_fence
          #define __atomic_signal_fence __mshqc_atomic_signal_fence
          #endif
          #endif
          EOF

            LIB_EXE=$(find "/c/Program Files/Microsoft Visual Studio" -name "lib.exe" | grep "Hostx64/x64" | head -n 1)

            # 1. Build libcint
            rm -rf /tmp/libcint
            git clone --depth 1 -b v6.1.2 https://github.com/sunqm/libcint.git /tmp/libcint
            "$CMAKE_EXE" -S /tmp/libcint -B /tmp/libcint/build -G "MinGW Makefiles" \
                  -DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX=/c/deps/Library \
                  -DCMAKE_C_COMPILER=/mingw64/bin/gcc.exe \
                  -DCMAKE_MAKE_PROGRAM=/mingw64/bin/mingw32-make.exe \
                  -DCMAKE_SHARED_LINKER_FLAGS="-Wl,--export-all-symbols -Wl,--output-def,C:/deps/Library/lib/cint.def" \
                  -DCMAKE_C_FLAGS="-O3 -mavx2 -mfma -mno-avx512f -funroll-loops -fno-math-errno -Wno-incompatible-pointer-types -std=gnu17" \
                  -DENABLE_EXAMPLE=0 -DENABLE_TEST=0 -DBUILD_SHARED_LIBS=ON
            "$CMAKE_EXE" --build /tmp/libcint/build -j4 --target install
            cp -f /c/deps/Library/lib/*.dll /c/deps/Library/bin/ 2>/dev/null || true
            cp -f /c/deps/Library/bin/libcint.dll /c/deps/Library/bin/cint.dll 2>/dev/null || true
            if [ -n "$LIB_EXE" ] && [ -f "/c/deps/Library/lib/cint.def" ]; then
              sed -i '1i LIBRARY "libcint.dll"' /c/deps/Library/lib/cint.def
              MSYS2_ARG_CONV_EXCL="*" "$LIB_EXE" /def:C:/deps/Library/lib/cint.def /out:C:/deps/Library/lib/cint.lib /machine:x64
            else
              cp -f /c/deps/Library/lib/libcint.dll.a /c/deps/Library/lib/cint.lib
            fi

            # 2. Build TBLIS
            rm -rf /tmp/tblis
            git clone --depth 1 -b v1.2.0 https://github.com/devinamatthews/tblis.git /tmp/tblis
            cd /tmp/tblis
            git submodule update --init --recursive
            sed -i 's/SIGSTKSZ/8192/g' src/external/catch/catch.hpp
            sed -i '1i #include <limits>' src/external/marray/include/short_vector.hpp
            sed -i '1i #include <limits>' src/external/marray/include/range.hpp
            sed -i '1i #include <limits>' src/util/thread.h
            sed -i '1i #include <stdlib.h>' src/external/tci/tci/task_set.c

            sed -i 's/extern const communicator single;/#ifdef _MSC_VER\nstatic const communicator single;\n#else\nextern const communicator single;\n#endif/g' src/util/thread.h
            sed -i 's|#include <x86intrin.h>|#if defined(__GNUC__) \&\& (defined(__x86_64__) \|\| defined(__i386__))\n#include <x86intrin.h>\n#else\n#undef __AVX512F__\n#undef __AVX__\n#undef __SSE2__\n#endif|g' src/external/marray/include/expression.hpp
            sed -i '/#include <cstdlib>/a #include <malloc.h>\nstatic inline int posix_memalign(void** p, size_t a, size_t s) { *p = _aligned_malloc(s, a); return *p ? 0 : 12; }' src/memory/aligned_allocator.hpp
            sed -i 's/free(ptr);/_aligned_free(ptr);/g' src/memory/aligned_allocator.hpp

            export CC=/mingw64/bin/gcc.exe
            export CXX=/mingw64/bin/g++.exe
            export CFLAGS="-O3 -mno-avx512f -mno-avx512vl -mno-avx512dq -mno-avx512bw -mno-avx512cd -Wno-incompatible-pointer-types -std=gnu17"
            export CXXFLAGS="-O3 -mno-avx512f -mno-avx512vl -mno-avx512dq -mno-avx512bw -mno-avx512cd -Wno-template-body -fpermissive -std=gnu++17"
            ./configure --prefix=/c/deps/Library --enable-static --disable-shared --without-hwloc \
                        --enable-thread-model=pthread \
                        --enable-config=haswell,zen,core2,sandybridge,reference \

            make install bin_PROGRAMS= noinst_PROGRAMS= check_PROGRAMS=

                  /mingw64/bin/libgomp-1.dll /mingw64/bin/libatomic-1.dll /mingw64/bin/libquadmath-0.dll \
                  /mingw64/bin/libgfortran-5.dll /c/deps/Library/bin/ 2>/dev/null || true

          fi

          python -c "import pathlib, re; p = pathlib.Path('C:/deps/Library/include/tci/yield.h'); p.write_text('#include <intrin.h>\n' + re.sub(r'__asm__[^;]*;', '_mm_pause();', p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')), encoding='utf-8') if p.exists() else None"
          python -c "import pathlib; p = pathlib.Path('C:/deps/Library/include/eigen3/unsupported/Eigen/CXX11/src/Tensor/Tensor.h'); p.write_text(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8').replace('{{firstDimension, otherDimensions...}}', '{{static_cast<Index>(firstDimension), static_cast<Index>(otherDimensions)...}}'), encoding='utf-8') if p.exists() else None"
          python -c "import pathlib; p = pathlib.Path('C:/deps/Library/include/eigen3/unsupported/Eigen/CXX11/src/Tensor/TensorDimensions.h'); p.write_text(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8').replace('{{firstDimension, secondDimension, otherDimensions...}}', '{{static_cast<DenseIndex>(firstDimension), static_cast<DenseIndex>(secondDimension), static_cast<DenseIndex>(otherDimensions)...}}'), encoding='utf-8') if p.exists() else None"
          python -c "import pathlib, re; [p.write_text('\n'.join(['// ' + l if re.match(r'^\s*#\s*pragma\s+omp\b', l) and ('max_Q_' in l or re.match(r'^\s*#\s*pragma\s+omp\s+simd\b', l)) else (re.sub(r'\bsimd\b', '', re.sub(r'\bcollapse\s*\(\s*\d+\s*\)', '', l)) if re.match(r'^\s*#\s*pragma\s+omp\b', l) else l) for l in p.read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines()]) + '\n', encoding='utf-8') for p in pathlib.Path('src').rglob('*.cc')]"

==> tests/test_mshqc.py <==
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Copyright 2026 Muhamad Syahrul Hidayat
#
# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
# You may obtain a copy of the License at
#
#     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
#
# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
# limitations under the License.

import pytest
import mshqc
import atexit

# Global list untuk menyimpan semua hasil pengujian
_TEST_RESULTS = []

def print_final_table():
    """Fungsi ini akan otomatis dipanggil di akhir eksekusi untuk mencetak tabel."""
    if not _TEST_RESULTS:
        return
        
    print("\n\n=======================================================================")
    print("                     FINAL TEST RESULTS SUMMARY")
    print("=======================================================================")
    print("-" * 65)
    print(f"| {'System':<8} | {'ERI Mode':<10} | {'Method':<8} | {'Total Energy (Ha)':<20} |")
    print("-" * 65)
    for r in _TEST_RESULTS:
        print(f"| {r[0]:<8} | {r[1]:<10} | {r[2]:<8} | {r[3]:<20.10f} |")
    print("-" * 65)

# Daftarkan fungsi print_final_table agar jalan otomatis saat script/pytest selesai
atexit.register(print_final_table)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("eri_mode", ["exact", "cholesky", "df"])
def test_closed_shell_h2o(eri_mode: str):
    """Test closed-shell H2O (RHF -> RMP2 -> OMP2 -> OMP3) with simplified bindings."""
    # 1. Initialize molecule
    mol = mshqc.Molecule(charge=0, multiplicity=1)
    mol.add_atom("O", 0.0000,  0.0000,  0.1173, units="angstrom")
    mol.add_atom("H", 0.0000,  0.7572, -0.4692, units="angstrom")
    mol.add_atom("H", 0.0000, -0.7572, -0.4692, units="angstrom")

    # 2. Load basis set and analytical integral engine
    basis     = mshqc.BasisSet("cc-pVTZ", mol)
    integrals = mshqc.IntegralEngine(mol, basis)

    # 3. Run RHF
    # Ubah baris ini di test_mshqc.py
    scf_conf = mshqc.SCFConfig(eri_method=eri_mode, threshold=1e-8, max_iterations=200)
    scf_res  = mshqc.RHF(mol, basis, integrals, scf_conf).compute()

    # 4. Run RMP2, OMP2, and OMP3 on the converged SCF reference
    mp2_conf = mshqc.MP2Config(eri_method=eri_mode, threshold=1e-9, gradient_threshold=1e-5)

    rmp2_res = mshqc.RMP2(mol, basis, integrals, scf_res, mp2_conf).compute()
    omp2_res = mshqc.OMP2(mol, basis, integrals, scf_res, mp2_conf).compute()
    omp3_res = mshqc.OMP3(mol, basis, integrals, scf_res, mp2_conf).compute()

    # Simpan hasil ke global list
    _TEST_RESULTS.append(("H2O", eri_mode.upper(), "RHF", scf_res.energy_total))
    _TEST_RESULTS.append(("H2O", eri_mode.upper(), "RMP2", rmp2_res.energy_total))
    _TEST_RESULTS.append(("H2O", eri_mode.upper(), "OMP2", omp2_res.energy_total))
    _TEST_RESULTS.append(("H2O", eri_mode.upper(), "OMP3", omp3_res.energy_total))

    print(f"\n[H2O | {eri_mode.upper()}]")
    print(f"RHF  Total Energy : {scf_res.energy_total:.10f} Ha")
    print(f"RMP2 Total Energy : {rmp2_res.energy_total:.10f} Ha (Corr: {rmp2_res.energy_mp2_corr:.10f} Ha)")
    print(f"OMP2 Total Energy : {omp2_res.energy_total:.10f} Ha")
    print(f"OMP3 Total Energy : {omp3_res.energy_total:.10f} Ha")

    assert scf_res.converged
    assert omp2_res.converged
    assert omp3_res.converged
    assert rmp2_res.energy_total < scf_res.energy_total
    assert omp2_res.energy_total < scf_res.energy_total
    assert omp3_res.energy_total < omp2_res.energy_total


@pytest.mark.parametrize("eri_mode", ["exact", "cholesky", "df"])
def test_open_shell_oxygen(eri_mode: str):
    """Test open-shell Oxygen (UHF -> UMP2 -> OMP2 -> OMP3) with simplified bindings."""
    # 1. Initialize triplet Oxygen atom (charge=0, multiplicity=3)
    mol = mshqc.Molecule(charge=0, multiplicity=3)
    mol.add_atom("O", 0.0000, 0.0000, 0.0000, units="angstrom")

    # 2. Load basis set and analytical integral engine
    basis     = mshqc.BasisSet("cc-pVTZ", mol)
    integrals = mshqc.IntegralEngine(mol, basis)

    # 3. Run UHF
    # Ubah baris ini di test_mshqc.py
    scf_conf = mshqc.SCFConfig(eri_method=eri_mode, threshold=1e-8, max_iterations=200)
    scf_res  = mshqc.UHF(mol, basis, integrals, scf_conf).compute()

    # 4. Run UMP2, OMP2, and OMP3 on the converged UHF reference
    mp2_conf = mshqc.MP2Config(eri_method=eri_mode, threshold=1e-9, gradient_threshold=1e-5)

    ump2_res = mshqc.UMP2(mol, basis, integrals, scf_res, mp2_conf).compute()
    omp2_res = mshqc.OMP2(mol, basis, integrals, scf_res, mp2_conf).compute()
    omp3_res = mshqc.OMP3(mol, basis, integrals, scf_res, mp2_conf).compute()

    # Simpan hasil ke global list
    _TEST_RESULTS.append(("Oxygen", eri_mode.upper(), "UHF", scf_res.energy_total))
    _TEST_RESULTS.append(("Oxygen", eri_mode.upper(), "UMP2", ump2_res.energy_total))
    _TEST_RESULTS.append(("Oxygen", eri_mode.upper(), "OMP2", omp2_res.energy_total))
    _TEST_RESULTS.append(("Oxygen", eri_mode.upper(), "OMP3", omp3_res.energy_total))

    print(f"\n[Oxygen | {eri_mode.upper()}]")
    print(f"UHF  Total Energy : {scf_res.energy_total:.10f} Ha")
    print(f"UMP2 Total Energy : {ump2_res.energy_total:.10f} Ha (Corr: {ump2_res.energy_mp2_corr:.10f} Ha)")
    print(f"OMP2 Total Energy : {omp2_res.energy_total:.10f} Ha")
    print(f"OMP3 Total Energy : {omp3_res.energy_total:.10f} Ha")

    assert scf_res.converged
    assert omp2_res.converged
    assert omp3_res.converged
    assert ump2_res.energy_total < scf_res.energy_total
    assert omp2_res.energy_total < scf_res.energy_total
    assert omp3_res.energy_total < omp2_res.energy_total


if __name__ == "__main__":
    for mode in ["exact", "cholesky", "df"]:
        test_closed_shell_h2o(mode)
        test_open_shell_oxygen(mode)

#include "mshqc/ints/integrals.h"

// Wajib dibungkus extern "C" karena libcint murni ditulis dalam C
extern "C" {
#include <cint.h>
int cint1e_ovlp_sph(double *buf, int *shls, int *atm, int natm, int *bas, int nbas, double *env, CINTOpt *opt);
int cint1e_kin_sph(double *buf, int *shls, int *atm, int natm, int *bas, int nbas, double *env, CINTOpt *opt);
int cint1e_nuc_sph(double *buf, int *shls, int *atm, int natm, int *bas, int nbas, double *env, CINTOpt *opt);
void CINTdel_optimizer(CINTOpt **opt);
int cint2c2e_sph(double *buf, int *shls, int *atm, int natm, int *bas, int nbas, double *env, CINTOpt *opt);
int cint3c2e_sph(double *buf, int *shls, int *atm, int natm, int *bas, int nbas, double *env, CINTOpt *opt);
}
#include <iostream>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

#ifdef _OPENMP
#include <omp.h>
#endif

namespace mshqc {

IntegralEngine::IntegralEngine(const Molecule& mol, const BasisSet& basis)
    : mol_(mol), basis_(basis), nbasis_(basis.n_basis_functions()), opt_(nullptr) {
    
    convert_basis_to_libcint(); 
    CINTOpt* tmp_opt = nullptr;
    cint2e_sph_optimizer(&tmp_opt, atm_.data(), mol_.n_atoms(), bas_.data(), basis_.n_shells(), env_.data());
    opt_ = static_cast<void*>(tmp_opt); 
    
    cache_valid = false;
}

IntegralEngine::~IntegralEngine() {
    if (opt_ != nullptr) {
        CINTOpt* tmp_opt = static_cast<CINTOpt*>(opt_);
        CINTdel_optimizer(&tmp_opt);
        opt_ = nullptr;
    }
}

int IntegralEngine::find_atom_index(const std::array<double, 3>& center) {
    int best_i = 0;
    double min_dist = 1e10; 
    for (size_t i = 0; i < mol_.n_atoms(); i++) {
        double dx = mol_.atom(i).x - center[0];
        double dy = mol_.atom(i).y - center[1];
        double dz = mol_.atom(i).z - center[2];
        double dist = std::sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz);
        if (dist < min_dist) {
            min_dist = dist;
            best_i = i;
        }
    }
    return best_i;
}
void IntegralEngine::convert_basis_to_libcint() {
    int natm = mol_.n_atoms();
    int nbas = basis_.n_shells();

    env_.assign(20, 0.0);
    atm_.resize(natm * ATM_SLOTS, 0);
    bas_.resize(nbas * BAS_SLOTS, 0);

    // 1. Pack Atoms
    for (int i = 0; i < natm; ++i) {
        atm_[i * ATM_SLOTS + CHARGE_OF] = mol_.atom(i).atomic_number;
        atm_[i * ATM_SLOTS + PTR_COORD] = env_.size();
        env_.push_back(mol_.atom(i).x);
        env_.push_back(mol_.atom(i).y);
        env_.push_back(mol_.atom(i).z);
    }

    for (int s = 0; s < nbas; ++s) {
        const auto& shell = basis_.shell(s);
        auto pos = shell.position(); 
        bas_[s * BAS_SLOTS + ATOM_OF]  = find_atom_index({pos[0], pos[1], pos[2]});
        bas_[s * BAS_SLOTS + ANG_OF]   = shell.l();
        bas_[s * BAS_SLOTS + NPRIM_OF] = shell.n_primitives();
        bas_[s * BAS_SLOTS + NCTR_OF]  = 1;
        bas_[s * BAS_SLOTS + PTR_EXP] = env_.size();
        for (size_t p = 0; p < shell.n_primitives(); ++p) {
            env_.push_back(shell.primitive(p).exponent);
        }
        bas_[s * BAS_SLOTS + PTR_COEFF] = env_.size();
        for (size_t p = 0; p < shell.n_primitives(); ++p) {
            double exp = shell.primitive(p).exponent;
            double coef = shell.primitive(p).coefficient;
            double norm = CINTgto_norm(shell.l(), exp);
            env_.push_back(coef * norm);
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// ONE-ELECTRON INTEGRALS
// ============================================================================
Eigen::MatrixXd IntegralEngine::compute_overlap() {
    Eigen::MatrixXd S = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nbasis_, nbasis_);
    auto shell2bf = basis_.shell_to_basis_function_map();
    int nbas = basis_.n_shells();

    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        std::vector<double> buf(10000);
        #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic)
        for (int s1 = 0; s1 < nbas; s1++) {
            for (int s2 = 0; s2 <= s1; s2++) {
                int shls[2] = {s1, s2};
                int has_val = cint1e_ovlp_sph(buf.data(), shls, atm_.data(), mol_.n_atoms(), bas_.data(), nbas, env_.data(), nullptr);
                if (!has_val) continue;

                int dim1 = CINTcgto_spheric(s1, bas_.data());
                int dim2 = CINTcgto_spheric(s2, bas_.data());
                int bf1 = shell2bf[s1];
                int bf2 = shell2bf[s2];

                for (int i = 0; i < dim1; ++i) {
                    for (int j = 0; j < dim2; ++j) {
                        double val = buf[i + j * dim1];
                        S(bf1 + i, bf2 + j) = val;
                        S(bf2 + j, bf1 + i) = val;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return S;
}

Eigen::MatrixXd IntegralEngine::compute_kinetic() {
    Eigen::MatrixXd T = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nbasis_, nbasis_);
    auto shell2bf = basis_.shell_to_basis_function_map();
    int nbas = basis_.n_shells();

    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        std::vector<double> buf(10000);
        #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic)
        for (int s1 = 0; s1 < nbas; s1++) {
            for (int s2 = 0; s2 <= s1; s2++) {
                int shls[2] = {s1, s2};
                int has_val = cint1e_kin_sph(buf.data(), shls, atm_.data(), mol_.n_atoms(), bas_.data(), nbas, env_.data(), nullptr);
                if (!has_val) continue;

                int dim1 = CINTcgto_spheric(s1, bas_.data());
                int dim2 = CINTcgto_spheric(s2, bas_.data());
                int bf1 = shell2bf[s1];
                int bf2 = shell2bf[s2];

                for (int i = 0; i < dim1; ++i) {
                    for (int j = 0; j < dim2; ++j) {
                        double val = buf[i + j * dim1];
                        T(bf1 + i, bf2 + j) = val;
                        T(bf2 + j, bf1 + i) = val;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return T;
}

Eigen::MatrixXd IntegralEngine::compute_nuclear() {
    Eigen::MatrixXd V = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nbasis_, nbasis_);
    auto shell2bf = basis_.shell_to_basis_function_map();
    int nbas = basis_.n_shells();

    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        std::vector<double> buf(10000);
        #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic)
        for (int s1 = 0; s1 < nbas; s1++) {
            for (int s2 = 0; s2 <= s1; s2++) {
                int shls[2] = {s1, s2};
                // [FIX 2] Tambahkan nullptr
                int has_val = cint1e_nuc_sph(buf.data(), shls, atm_.data(), mol_.n_atoms(), bas_.data(), nbas, env_.data(), nullptr);
                if (!has_val) continue;

                int dim1 = CINTcgto_spheric(s1, bas_.data());
                int dim2 = CINTcgto_spheric(s2, bas_.data());
                int bf1 = shell2bf[s1];
                int bf2 = shell2bf[s2];

                for (int i = 0; i < dim1; ++i) {
                    for (int j = 0; j < dim2; ++j) {
                        double val = buf[i + j * dim1];
                        V(bf1 + i, bf2 + j) = val;
                        V(bf2 + j, bf1 + i) = val;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return V;
}

Eigen::MatrixXd IntegralEngine::compute_core_hamiltonian() {
    return compute_kinetic() + compute_nuclear();
}

// ============================================================================
// COMPUTE ERI DENSE (TWO-ELECTRON)
// ============================================================================
const Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>& IntegralEngine::compute_eri() {
    if (cache_valid) return cached_eri;

    cached_eri.resize(nbasis_, nbasis_, nbasis_, nbasis_);
    cached_eri.setZero();
    auto shell2bf = basis_.shell_to_basis_function_map();
    int nbas = basis_.n_shells();
    std::vector<double> schwarz_max(nbas * nbas, 0.0);
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        std::vector<double> buf(65536, 0.0);
        #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic)
        for (int s1 = 0; s1 < nbas; ++s1) {
            for (int s2 = 0; s2 <= s1; ++s2) {
                int dim1 = CINTcgto_spheric(s1, bas_.data());
                int dim2 = CINTcgto_spheric(s2, bas_.data());
                size_t req_sz = static_cast<size_t>(dim1) * dim2 * dim1 * dim2;
                if (buf.size() < req_sz) buf.resize(req_sz);

                int shls[4] = {s1, s2, s1, s2};
                int has_val = cint2e_sph(buf.data(), shls, atm_.data(), mol_.n_atoms(), bas_.data(), nbas, env_.data(), static_cast<CINTOpt*>(opt_));
                if (has_val) {
                    double max_v = 0.0;
                    for (size_t x = 0; x < req_sz; ++x) max_v = std::max(max_v, std::abs(buf[x]));
                    schwarz_max[s1 * nbas + s2] = std::sqrt(max_v);
                    schwarz_max[s2 * nbas + s1] = std::sqrt(max_v);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // 2. Main ERI Loop
    const double screen_thresh = 1e-12;
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        std::vector<double> buf(65536, 0.0);
        #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
        for (int s1 = 0; s1 < nbas; ++s1) {
            for (int s2 = 0; s2 <= s1; ++s2) {
                double bound1 = schwarz_max[s1 * nbas + s2];
                if (bound1 < screen_thresh) continue;

                for (int s3 = 0; s3 <= s1; ++s3) {
                    int s4_max = (s1 == s3) ? s2 : s3;
                    for (int s4 = 0; s4 <= s4_max; ++s4) {
                        if (bound1 * schwarz_max[s3 * nbas + s4] < screen_thresh) continue;

                        int dim1 = CINTcgto_spheric(s1, bas_.data());
                        int dim2 = CINTcgto_spheric(s2, bas_.data());
                        int dim3 = CINTcgto_spheric(s3, bas_.data());
                        int dim4 = CINTcgto_spheric(s4, bas_.data());
                        size_t req_sz = static_cast<size_t>(dim1) * dim2 * dim3 * dim4;
                        if (buf.size() < req_sz) buf.resize(req_sz);

                        int shls[4] = {s1, s2, s3, s4};
                        if (!cint2e_sph(buf.data(), shls, atm_.data(), mol_.n_atoms(), bas_.data(), nbas, env_.data(), static_cast<CINTOpt*>(opt_))) continue;

                        int bf1 = shell2bf[s1]; int bf2 = shell2bf[s2];
                        int bf3 = shell2bf[s3]; int bf4 = shell2bf[s4];

                        // Konversi layout Col-Major libcint (i fast) -> C-Major (l fast)
                        for (int i = 0; i < dim1; ++i) {
                            for (int j = 0; j < dim2; ++j) {
                                for (int k = 0; k < dim3; ++k) {
                                    for (int l = 0; l < dim4; ++l) {
                                        double val = buf[i + dim1*(j + dim2*(k + dim3*l))];

                                        Eigen::Index i1 = bf1 + i, i2 = bf2 + j, i3 = bf3 + k, i4 = bf4 + l;
                                        cached_eri(i1, i2, i3, i4) = val;
                                        cached_eri(i2, i1, i3, i4) = val;
                                        cached_eri(i1, i2, i4, i3) = val;
                                        cached_eri(i2, i1, i4, i3) = val;
                                        cached_eri(i3, i4, i1, i2) = val;
                                        cached_eri(i4, i3, i1, i2) = val;
                                        cached_eri(i3, i4, i2, i1) = val;
                                        cached_eri(i4, i3, i2, i1) = val;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    cache_valid = true;
    return cached_eri;
}

// ============================================================================
// SINGLE SHELL BLOCK COMPUTE (THREAD-SAFE)
// ============================================================================
std::vector<double> IntegralEngine::compute_shell_block(int sh_a, int sh_b, int sh_c, int sh_d) {
    // Alokasi memori lokal per pemanggilan fungsi, mengeliminasi thread_local
    // untuk mencegah race condition pada akses OpenMP konkuren.
    std::vector<double> t_buffer;
    
    int dim1 = CINTcgto_spheric(sh_a, bas_.data());
    int dim2 = CINTcgto_spheric(sh_b, bas_.data());
    int dim3 = CINTcgto_spheric(sh_c, bas_.data());
    int dim4 = CINTcgto_spheric(sh_d, bas_.data());
    size_t sz = dim1 * dim2 * dim3 * dim4;
    
    if (t_buffer.size() < sz) t_buffer.resize(sz);
    
    int shls[4] = {sh_a, sh_b, sh_c, sh_d};
    
    CINTOpt* tmp_opt = static_cast<CINTOpt*>(opt_);
    int has_val = cint2e_sph(t_buffer.data(), shls, atm_.data(), mol_.n_atoms(), bas_.data(), basis_.n_shells(), env_.data(), tmp_opt);
    
    if (!has_val) {
        std::fill(t_buffer.begin(), t_buffer.begin() + sz, 0.0);
    }
    
    return t_buffer;
}

int IntegralEngine::compute_shell_block_raw(int sh_a, int sh_b, int sh_c, int sh_d, double* buf_out) const {
    int shls[4] = {sh_a, sh_b, sh_c, sh_d};
    CINTOpt* tmp_opt = static_cast<CINTOpt*>(opt_);
    return cint2e_sph(buf_out, shls,
                      const_cast<int*>(atm_.data()), mol_.n_atoms(),
                      const_cast<int*>(bas_.data()), basis_.n_shells(),
                      const_cast<double*>(env_.data()), tmp_opt);
}

const double* IntegralEngine::compute_shell_block_ptr(int sh_a, int sh_b, int sh_c, int sh_d, size_t& size_out) {
    static thread_local std::vector<double> tl_buf;
    tl_buf = compute_shell_block(sh_a, sh_b, sh_c, sh_d);
    size_out = tl_buf.size();
    return tl_buf.data();
}
// ============================================================================
// DENSITY FITTING INTEGRALS (2-Center & 3-Center)
// ============================================================================

std::vector<double> IntegralEngine::compute_2c2e_block(int sh_P, int sh_Q) {
    std::vector<double> t_buffer;
    
    int dimP = CINTcgto_spheric(sh_P, bas_.data());
    int dimQ = CINTcgto_spheric(sh_Q, bas_.data());
    size_t sz = dimP * dimQ;
    
    if (t_buffer.size() < sz) t_buffer.resize(sz);
    
    int shls[2] = {sh_P, sh_Q};
    CINTOpt* tmp_opt = static_cast<CINTOpt*>(opt_); // Memakai optimizer Libcint
    
    // Panggil Libcint 2-center 2-electron (TANPA OPTIMIZER)
    int has_val = cint2c2e_sph(t_buffer.data(), shls, atm_.data(), mol_.n_atoms(), bas_.data(), basis_.n_shells(), env_.data(), nullptr);
    
    if (!has_val) {
        std::fill(t_buffer.begin(), t_buffer.begin() + sz, 0.0);
    }
    
    return t_buffer;
}

std::vector<double> IntegralEngine::compute_3c2e_block(int sh_i, int sh_j, int sh_P) {
    std::vector<double> t_buffer;
    
    int dim1 = CINTcgto_spheric(sh_i, bas_.data());
    int dim2 = CINTcgto_spheric(sh_j, bas_.data());
    int dimP = CINTcgto_spheric(sh_P, bas_.data());
    size_t sz = dim1 * dim2 * dimP;
    
    if (t_buffer.size() < sz) t_buffer.resize(sz);
    int shls[3] = {sh_i, sh_j, sh_P};
    CINTOpt* tmp_opt = static_cast<CINTOpt*>(opt_);
    int has_val = cint3c2e_sph(t_buffer.data(), shls, atm_.data(), mol_.n_atoms(), bas_.data(), basis_.n_shells(), env_.data(), nullptr);
    
    if (!has_val) {
        std::fill(t_buffer.begin(), t_buffer.begin() + sz, 0.0);
    }
    
    return t_buffer;
}

int IntegralEngine::compute_2c2e_block_raw(int sh_P, int sh_Q, double* buf_out) const {
    int shls[2] = {sh_P, sh_Q};
    return cint2c2e_sph(buf_out, shls,
                        const_cast<int*>(atm_.data()), mol_.n_atoms(),
                        const_cast<int*>(bas_.data()), basis_.n_shells(),
                        const_cast<double*>(env_.data()), nullptr);
}

int IntegralEngine::compute_3c2e_block_raw(int sh_i, int sh_j, int sh_P, double* buf_out) const {
    int shls[3] = {sh_i, sh_j, sh_P};
    return cint3c2e_sph(buf_out, shls,
                        const_cast<int*>(atm_.data()), mol_.n_atoms(),
                        const_cast<int*>(bas_.data()), basis_.n_shells(),
                        const_cast<double*>(env_.data()), nullptr);
}

Eigen::MatrixXd IntegralEngine::compute_2center_eri(const BasisSet&) { return Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(1,1); }
double IntegralEngine::compute_single_eri(int, int, int, int) { return 0.0; }
Eigen::VectorXd IntegralEngine::compute_eri_diagonal() { return Eigen::VectorXd::Zero(nbasis_); }
Eigen::VectorXd IntegralEngine::compute_eri_column(int) { return Eigen::VectorXd::Zero(nbasis_); }

/**
 * @file screening.cc
 * @brief Implementation of Shell-Based Schwarz Screening
 */

#include "mshqc/integrals/screening.h"
#include "mshqc/ints/integrals.h" 
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>
#include <iostream>
#include <iomanip>

#ifdef _OPENMP
#include <omp.h>
#ifdef I
#undef I
#endif
#endif

namespace mshqc {
namespace integrals {

Screening::Screening(const BasisSet& basis) 
    : basis_(basis), nshells_(basis.n_shells()), max_Q_(0.0) 
{
    Q_matrix_ = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nshells_, nshells_);
}

void Screening::reset() {
    max_Q_ = 0.0;
    Q_matrix_.setZero();
}

// Implementasi compute sekarang akan dikenali karena sudah ada di header
void Screening::compute(std::shared_ptr<mshqc::IntegralEngine> integrals) {
    if (!integrals) throw std::runtime_error("IntegralEngine null in Screening::compute");

    max_Q_ = 0.0;
    
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic) reduction(max:max_Q_)
    for (int i = 0; i < nshells_; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            // Memanggil fungsi dari IntegralEngine
            auto buffer = integrals->compute_shell_block(i, j, i, j);
            
            double max_val = 0.0;
            for (double v : buffer) {
                max_val = std::max(max_val, std::abs(v));
            }
            
            double Q_val = std::sqrt(max_val);
            
            Q_matrix_(i, j) = Q_val;
            Q_matrix_(j, i) = Q_val; 
            
            if (Q_val > max_Q_) max_Q_ = Q_val;
        }
    }

    if (max_Q_ < 1e-12) max_Q_ = 1.0;
}

std::vector<ShellPair> Screening::get_significant_pairs(double threshold) const {
    std::vector<ShellPair> pairs;
    pairs.reserve(nshells_ * nshells_ / 4);

    double safe_max_Q = (max_Q_ > 1e-12) ? max_Q_ : 1.0;
    double effective_cutoff = threshold / safe_max_Q;

    for (int i = 0; i < nshells_; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double val = Q_matrix_(i, j);
            if (val >= effective_cutoff) {
                pairs.push_back({i, j, val});
            }
        }
    }

    std::sort(pairs.begin(), pairs.end(), 
        [](const ShellPair& a, const ShellPair& b) {
            return a.max_val > b.max_val; 
        });

    return pairs;
}

bool Screening::is_significant(int sh_a, int sh_b, int sh_c, int sh_d, double threshold) const {
    double bound = Q_matrix_(sh_a, sh_b) * Q_matrix_(sh_c, sh_d);
    return bound >= threshold;
}

double Screening::get_schwarz_val(int sh_a, int sh_b) const {
    if (sh_a >= nshells_ || sh_b >= nshells_) return 0.0;
    return Q_matrix_(sh_a, sh_b);
}

void Screening::print_stats(double threshold) const {
    long long total_pairs = (long long)nshells_ * (nshells_ + 1) / 2;
    long long kept_pairs = 0;
    
    double safe_max_Q = (max_Q_ > 1e-12) ? max_Q_ : 1.0;
    double effective_cutoff = threshold / safe_max_Q;

    for (int i = 0; i < nshells_; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            if (Q_matrix_(i, j) >= effective_cutoff) {
                kept_pairs++;
            }
        }
    }

    double percent = 100.0 * kept_pairs / total_pairs;
    
    std::cout << "\n  [Screening Stats]" << std::endl;
    std::cout << "  Threshold       : " << std::scientific << threshold << std::endl;
    std::cout << "  Max Schwarz (Q) : " << max_Q_ << std::endl;
    std::cout << "  Shell Pairs     : " << kept_pairs << " / " << total_pairs 
              << " (" << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << percent << "% kept)" << std::endl;
    std::cout << "  Sparsity Gain   : " << (100.0 - percent) << "% computations skipped.\n" << std::endl;
}

// Stub function untuk shell_centers_ (jika belum dipakai, dikosongkan dulu)
void Screening::init_shell_centers() {}

void Screening::build_from_eri_tensor(const Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>& eri) {
    // Implementasi placeholder jika diperlukan, atau bisa dihapus
}

/**
 * @file src/integrals/cholesky_eri.cc
 * @brief DIRECT Cholesky Decomposition (Hybrid: Optimized Tensor & Screened Direct)
 * @details 
 * - FIXED: Tensor input diproses dengan benar (tidak di-skip).
 * - OPTIMIZED: Loop Tensor menggunakan OpenMP collapse untuk kecepatan tinggi.
 * - OPTIMIZED: Mode Direct menggunakan Shell-Screening.
 */

#include "mshqc/integrals/cholesky_eri.h"
#include "mshqc/utils/hdf5_io.h"
#include <iostream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <cmath>
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <omp.h>
#ifdef I
#undef I
#endif

namespace mshqc {
namespace integrals {





CholeskyERI::CholeskyERI(double threshold)
    : n_basis_(0), n_vectors_(0), decomposed_(false) 
{
    config_.threshold = threshold;
    threshold_ = threshold;
}

CholeskyERI::CholeskyERI(const BasisSet& basis, std::shared_ptr<IntegralEngine> integrals)
    : basis_ptr_(&basis), integrals_ptr_(integrals), 
      n_basis_(basis.n_basis_functions()), n_vectors_(0), decomposed_(false) 
{
    config_.threshold = 1e-6;
    config_.print_level = 1;
    threshold_ = 1e-6;

    
    bf2shell_.resize(n_basis_);
    int current_func = 0;
    for(int s = 0; s < basis.n_shells(); ++s) {
        int count = basis.shell(s).n_functions();
        for(int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
            if (current_func + i < n_basis_) {
                bf2shell_[current_func + i] = s;
            }
        }
        current_func += count;
    }
}





void CholeskyERI::compute() {
    if (!integrals_ptr_ || !basis_ptr_) {
        std::cerr << "[Error] CholeskyERI: Missing Engine/Basis! Cannot run compute().\n";
        return;
    }

    decompose_direct();
}

CholeskyDecompositionResult CholeskyERI::decompose(const Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>& eri_full) {
    
    auto dims = eri_full.dimensions();
    n_basis_ = static_cast<int>(dims[0]);
    int n_pairs = n_basis_ * n_basis_;
    
    if (config_.print_level > 0) {
        std::cout << "\n======================================================================\n";
        std::cout << "  Cholesky Decomposition (Tensor Mode - Exact)\n";
        std::cout << "======================================================================\n";
        std::cout << "  Basis: " << n_basis_ << " | Pairs: " << n_pairs << "\n";
    }

    reset();

    
    Eigen::VectorXd D(n_pairs);
    
    
    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2)
    for (int i = 0; i < n_basis_; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j < n_basis_; ++j) {
            D(i * n_basis_ + j) = eri_full(i, j, i, j);
        }
    }

    double max_diag = D.maxCoeff();
    if (config_.print_level > 0) 
        std::cout << "  Max Diag: " << std::scientific << max_diag << " | Threshold: " << threshold_ << "\n";

    
    int est_rank = std::min(n_pairs, std::max(200, n_basis_ * 5));
    Eigen::MatrixXd L_store(n_pairs, est_rank);
    
    int iter = 0;
    while (true) {
        
        int pivot_idx;
        double D_max = D.maxCoeff(&pivot_idx);

        if (D_max < threshold_ || iter >= n_pairs) break;

        
        if (iter >= L_store.cols()) {
            L_store.conservativeResize(Eigen::NoChange, L_store.cols() * 2);
        }

        double inv_sqrt = 1.0 / std::sqrt(D_max);
        int pi = pivot_idx / n_basis_;
        int pj = pivot_idx % n_basis_;

        
        
        #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2)
        for (int i = 0; i < n_basis_; ++i) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n_basis_; ++j) {
                L_store(i * n_basis_ + j, iter) = eri_full(i, j, pi, pj);
            }
        }

        
        if (iter > 0) {
            
            L_store.col(iter) -= L_store.leftCols(iter) * L_store.row(pivot_idx).head(iter).transpose();
        }

        
        L_store.col(iter) *= inv_sqrt;
        D.array() -= L_store.col(iter).array().square();

        
        
        for(int k=0; k<n_pairs; ++k) if (D(k) < 0.0) D(k) = 0.0;

        iter++;
    }

    n_vectors_ = iter;
    decomposed_ = true;

    L_mat_ = L_store.leftCols(n_vectors_);

    if (config_.print_level > 0) {
        std::cout << "  Done. Vectors: " << n_vectors_ 
                  << " | Ratio: " << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << compression_ratio() << "x\n";
    }

    CholeskyDecompositionResult res;
    res.n_vectors = n_vectors_;
    res.converged = true;
    res.max_error = D.maxCoeff();
    return res;
}




void CholeskyERI::decompose_direct() {
    if (!integrals_ptr_ || !basis_ptr_) return;
    const auto& basis = *basis_ptr_;
    int nshells = basis.n_shells();
    int npair = n_basis_ * n_basis_;
    reset();

    std::vector<int> shell_starts(nshells);
    int offset = 0;
    for (int s = 0; s < nshells; ++s) {
        shell_starts[s] = offset;
        offset += basis.shell(s).n_functions();
    }

    Eigen::VectorXd D(npair);
    D.setZero();
    std::vector<double> shell_max(nshells * nshells, 0.0);
    std::vector<std::pair<int, int>> valid_pairs;

    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1)
    for (int s1 = 0; s1 < nshells; ++s1) {
        for (int s2 = 0; s2 <= s1; ++s2) {
            const auto& buf = integrals_ptr_->compute_shell_block(s1, s2, s1, s2);
            if (buf.empty()) continue;

            int dim1 = basis.shell(s1).n_functions();
            int dim2 = basis.shell(s2).n_functions();
            int st1 = shell_starts[s1];
            int st2 = shell_starts[s2];
            double max_val_block = 0.0;

            for (int i = 0; i < dim1; ++i) {
                for (int j = 0; j < dim2; ++j) {
                    size_t idx_buf = i + dim1 * (j + dim2 * (i + dim1 * j));
                    if (idx_buf >= buf.size()) continue;

                    double val = buf[idx_buf];
                    int p = st1 + i;
                    int q = st2 + j;

                    if (p * n_basis_ + q < npair) D(p * n_basis_ + q) = val;
                    if (q * n_basis_ + p < npair) D(q * n_basis_ + p) = val;
                    max_val_block = std::max(max_val_block, std::abs(val));
                }
            }
            double s_max = std::sqrt(max_val_block);
            shell_max[s1 * nshells + s2] = s_max;
            shell_max[s2 * nshells + s1] = s_max;

            if (s_max > 1e-15) {
                #pragma omp critical
                valid_pairs.push_back({s1, s2});
            }
        }
    }

    int est_rank = std::min(npair, std::max(200, n_basis_ * 5));
    Eigen::MatrixXd L_store(npair, est_rank);

    // Cutoff screening diperketat ke 1e-15 agar tidak teramplifikasi oleh 1/sqrt(D_max)
    const double screen_cutoff = std::min(1e-15, threshold_ * 1e-6);

    int n_shell_pairs = nshells * (nshells + 1) / 2;
    std::vector<Eigen::MatrixXd> shell_pair_cache(n_shell_pairs);

    int iter = 0;
    while (true) {
        int pivot_idx = -1;
        double D_max = D.maxCoeff(&pivot_idx);

        if (D_max < threshold_ || pivot_idx == -1 || iter >= npair) break;
        if (iter >= L_store.cols()) {
            L_store.conservativeResize(Eigen::NoChange, L_store.cols() * 2);
        }

        int p = pivot_idx / n_basis_;
        int q = pivot_idx % n_basis_;
        if (p < q) std::swap(p, q);

        int sp = bf2shell_[p];
        int sq = bf2shell_[q];
        int rel_p = p - shell_starts[sp];
        int rel_q = q - shell_starts[sq];
        int pair_pq = sp * (sp + 1) / 2 + sq;
        int dimP = basis.shell(sp).n_functions();
        int dimQ = basis.shell(sq).n_functions();

        if (shell_pair_cache[pair_pq].rows() == 0) {
            Eigen::MatrixXd blk_mat = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(npair, dimP * dimQ);
            double pivot_schwarz = shell_max[sp * nshells + sq];

            #pragma omp parallel
            {
                std::vector<double> thread_buf(10000);
                #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 16) nowait
                for (size_t v = 0; v < valid_pairs.size(); ++v) {
                    int s1 = valid_pairs[v].first;
                    int s2 = valid_pairs[v].second;
                    if (shell_max[s1 * nshells + s2] * pivot_schwarz < screen_cutoff) continue;

                    int pair_s = s1 * (s1 + 1) / 2 + s2;
                    bool swap_pairs = (pair_s < pair_pq);
                    int u1 = swap_pairs ? sp : s1;
                    int u2 = swap_pairs ? sq : s2;
                    int u3 = swap_pairs ? s1 : sp;
                    int u4 = swap_pairs ? s2 : sq;

                    if (!integrals_ptr_->compute_shell_block_raw(u1, u2, u3, u4, thread_buf.data())) continue;

                    int dim1 = basis.shell(s1).n_functions();
                    int dim2 = basis.shell(s2).n_functions();
                    int st1 = shell_starts[s1];
                    int st2 = shell_starts[s2];

                    for (int rp = 0; rp < dimP; ++rp) {
                        for (int rq = 0; rq < dimQ; ++rq) {
                            int col_pq = rp + dimP * rq;
                            for (int i = 0; i < dim1; ++i) {
                                int global_i = st1 + i;
                                for (int j = 0; j < dim2; ++j) {
                                    int global_j = st2 + j;
                                    size_t idx_buf = (!swap_pairs)
                                        ? (i + dim1 * (j + dim2 * (rp + dimP * rq)))
                                        : (rp + dimP * (rq + dimQ * (i + dim1 * j)));
                                    double val = thread_buf[idx_buf];
                                    blk_mat(global_i * n_basis_ + global_j, col_pq) = val;
                                    if (global_i != global_j) blk_mat(global_j * n_basis_ + global_i, col_pq) = val;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            shell_pair_cache[pair_pq] = std::move(blk_mat);
        }

        Eigen::VectorXd col_buf = shell_pair_cache[pair_pq].col(rel_p + dimP * rel_q);

        if (iter > 0) {
            Eigen::Map<const Eigen::MatrixXd> L_prev(L_store.data(), npair, iter);
            Eigen::VectorXd L_piv = L_prev.row(pivot_idx).transpose();
            #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
            for (int r = 0; r < npair; r += 256) {
                int r_len = std::min(256, npair - r);
                col_buf.segment(r, r_len).noalias() -= L_prev.middleRows(r, r_len) * L_piv;
            }
        }

        double exact_diag = col_buf(pivot_idx);
        if (exact_diag < threshold_) {
            D(p * n_basis_ + q) = 0.0;
            D(q * n_basis_ + p) = 0.0;
            shell_pair_cache[pair_pq].resize(0, 0);
            continue;
        }
        double inv_sqrt = 1.0 / std::sqrt(exact_diag);

        L_store.col(iter) = col_buf * inv_sqrt;
        D.array() -= L_store.col(iter).array().square();

        D(p * n_basis_ + q) = 0.0;
        D(q * n_basis_ + p) = 0.0;
        D = D.cwiseMax(0.0);

       
        double max_rem_in_shell = 0.0;
        int stP = shell_starts[sp], stQ = shell_starts[sq];
        for (int rp = 0; rp < dimP; ++rp) {
            for (int rq = 0; rq < dimQ; ++rq) {
                max_rem_in_shell = std::max(max_rem_in_shell, D((stP + rp) * n_basis_ + (stQ + rq)));
            }
        }
        if (max_rem_in_shell < std::max(threshold_, 0.1 * D_max)) {
            shell_pair_cache[pair_pq].resize(0, 0);
        }

        iter++;
    }

    shell_pair_cache.clear();
    shell_pair_cache.shrink_to_fit();
    n_vectors_ = iter;
    decomposed_ = true;
    L_store.conservativeResize(Eigen::NoChange, n_vectors_);
    L_mat_ = std::move(L_store);
}


void CholeskyERI::reset() {
    L_mat_.resize(0, 0);
    n_vectors_ = 0;
    decomposed_ = false;
}

double CholeskyERI::compression_ratio() const {
    if (n_basis_ == 0) return 0.0;
    double full_size = std::pow(n_basis_, 4);
    double chol_size = std::max(1.0, (double)n_vectors_ * n_basis_ * n_basis_);
    return full_size / chol_size;
}

double CholeskyERI::reconstruct(int i, int j, int k, int l) const {
    if (!decomposed_) return 0.0;
    int ij = i * n_basis_ + j;
    int kl = k * n_basis_ + l;
    return L_mat_.row(ij).dot(L_mat_.row(kl)); 
}

size_t CholeskyERI::storage_bytes() const {
    return L_mat_.size() * sizeof(double);
}

void CholeskyERI::save_to_hdf5(const std::string& filename, const std::string& dataset_name) const {
    if (!decomposed_ || n_vectors_ == 0) return;

    utils::HDF5TensorIO io(filename, utils::HDF5TensorIO::Mode::WRITE_TRUNCATE);
    long n_vec = n_vectors_;
    
    io.create_dataset_4d(dataset_name, {n_vec, n_basis_, n_basis_, 1}, {1, n_basis_, n_basis_, 1});

    for (long k = 0; k < n_vec; ++k) {
        std::array<long, 4> offset = {k, 0, 0, 0};
        std::array<long, 4> slice_dims = {1, n_basis_, n_basis_, 1};
        
        io.write_slice_4d(dataset_name, offset, slice_dims, L_mat_.col(k).data());
    }
}

void CholeskyERI::load_from_hdf5(const std::string& filename, const std::string& dataset_name) {
    utils::HDF5TensorIO io(filename, utils::HDF5TensorIO::Mode::READ_ONLY);
    
    Eigen::Tensor<double, 4> tensor_in = io.read_tensor_4d(dataset_name);
    
    long n_vec = tensor_in.dimension(0);
    n_basis_ = static_cast<int>(tensor_in.dimension(1));
    long n_pairs = n_basis_ * n_basis_;
    
    n_vectors_ = static_cast<int>(n_vec);
    
    
    L_mat_.resize(n_pairs, n_vectors_);
    
    for (int k = 0; k < n_vectors_; ++k) {
        std::array<long, 4> offset = {k, 0, 0, 0};
        std::array<long, 4> slice_dims = {1, n_basis_, n_basis_, 1};
        
        io.read_slice_4d(dataset_name, offset, slice_dims, L_mat_.col(k).data());
    }
    decomposed_ = true;
}


Eigen::Tensor<double, 4> CholeskyERI::reconstruct_full() const { return Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>(); }
std::pair<double, double> CholeskyERI::validate_reconstruction(const Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>&) { return {0.0, 0.0}; }
void CholeskyERI::print_statistics(bool) const {}
int CholeskyERI::find_pivot(const Eigen::VectorXd&) const { return 0; }
Eigen::VectorXd CholeskyERI::compute_new_vector(const Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>&, const Eigen::VectorXd&, int) const { return Eigen::VectorXd(); }

}

/**
 * @file src/integrals/df_eri.cc
 * @brief Implementasi Density Fitting Tensor Generator
 */

#include "mshqc/integrals/df_eri.h"
#include "mshqc/utils/hdf5_io.h"
#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <omp.h>

namespace mshqc {
namespace integrals {

DensityFittingERI::DensityFittingERI(const BasisSet& primary_basis, 
                                     const BasisSet& aux_basis, 
                                     std::shared_ptr<IntegralEngine> integrals,
                                     double cutoff)
    : primary_basis_(&primary_basis), 
      aux_basis_(&aux_basis), 
      integrals_(integrals),
      is_computed_(false),
      cutoff_(cutoff) 
{
    n_primary_ = primary_basis_->n_basis_functions();
    n_aux_     = aux_basis_->n_basis_functions();
}

struct CholeskyResult {
    Eigen::MatrixXd L;
    std::vector<int> pivot_map;
    int rank;
};

CholeskyResult compute_pivoted_cholesky(Eigen::MatrixXd& J, double tol = 1e-10) {
    int n = J.rows();
    std::vector<int> p(n);
    std::iota(p.begin(), p.end(), 0); 
    
    Eigen::VectorXd diag = J.diagonal();
    Eigen::MatrixXd L = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(n, n);
    
    int rank = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        
        int max_idx = i;
        double max_val = diag(i);
        for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            if (diag(j) > max_val) {
                max_val = diag(j);
                max_idx = j;
            }
        }
        
      
        if (max_val < tol) break; 
        
       
        if (max_idx != i) {
            std::swap(p[i], p[max_idx]);
            std::swap(diag(i), diag(max_idx));
            J.row(i).swap(J.row(max_idx));
            J.col(i).swap(J.col(max_idx));
            L.row(i).swap(L.row(max_idx)); 
        }
        
        L(i, i) = std::sqrt(diag(i));
        double L_ii_inv = 1.0 / L(i, i);
        
        int rem = n - i - 1;
        if (rem > 0) {
            if (i > 0) {
                L.col(i).tail(rem).noalias() = J.col(i).tail(rem)
                    - L.bottomLeftCorner(rem, i) * L.row(i).head(i).transpose();
            } else {
                L.col(i).tail(rem) = J.col(i).tail(rem);
            }
            L.col(i).tail(rem) *= L_ii_inv;
            diag.tail(rem).array() -= L.col(i).tail(rem).array().square();
        }
        rank++;
    }

    L.conservativeResize(n, rank);
    return {L, p, rank};
}
void DensityFittingERI::compute(bool save_hdf5) {
    std::cout << "  Starting Density Fitting (RI) Decomposition...\n";
    std::cout << "  Primary Basis: " << n_primary_ << " BF\n";
    std::cout << "  Auxiliary Basis: " << n_aux_ << " BF\n";

    int n_shells_prim = primary_basis_->n_shells();
    int n_shells_aux  = aux_basis_->n_shells();

    // 1. Setup offset arrays untuk auxiliary basis
    std::vector<int> aux_starts(n_shells_aux), aux_sizes(n_shells_aux);
    int offset_a = 0;
    for(int i = 0; i < n_shells_aux; ++i) {
        aux_starts[i] = offset_a;
        aux_sizes[i] = aux_basis_->shell(i).n_functions();
        offset_a += aux_sizes[i];
    }

    // Setup offset arrays untuk primary basis
    std::vector<int> prim_starts(n_shells_prim), prim_sizes(n_shells_prim);
    int offset_p = 0;
    for(int i = 0; i < n_shells_prim; ++i) {
        prim_starts[i] = offset_p;
        prim_sizes[i] = primary_basis_->shell(i).n_functions();
        offset_p += prim_sizes[i];
    }

    // =====================================================================
    // 2. REKONSTRUKSI MATRIKS METRIK J BERBASIS SIMD & OPENMP
    // =====================================================================
    std::cout << "  Evaluating 2-Center Metric Matrix...\n";
    Eigen::MatrixXd J_metric = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(n_aux_, n_aux_);

    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        std::vector<double> buffer(10000);
        #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1) nowait
        for (int R = 0; R < n_shells_aux; ++R) {
            int abs_R = n_shells_prim + R; 
            int dim_R = aux_sizes[R];
            int bf_R_start = aux_starts[R];

            for (int S = 0; S <= R; ++S) {
                int abs_S = n_shells_prim + S;
                int dim_S = aux_sizes[S];
                int bf_S_start = aux_starts[S];

                if (!integrals_->compute_2c2e_block_raw(abs_R, abs_S, buffer.data())) continue;

                for (int s = 0; s < dim_S; ++s) {
                    int idx_S = bf_S_start + s;
                    int buffer_offset = dim_R * s;
                    for (int r = 0; r < dim_R; ++r) {
                        int idx_R = bf_R_start + r;
                        double val = buffer[r + buffer_offset];
                        
                        J_metric(idx_R, idx_S) = val;
                        if (idx_R != idx_S) J_metric(idx_S, idx_R) = val;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    // =====================================================================
    // 3. DEKOMPOSISI EIGEN METRIK SIMETRIS J^{-1/2} (IDENTIK DENGAN PSI4)
    // =====================================================================
    Eigen::SelfAdjointEigenSolver<Eigen::MatrixXd> es(J_metric);
    const Eigen::VectorXd& evals = es.eigenvalues();
    const Eigen::MatrixXd& evecs = es.eigenvectors();

    double eig_tol = std::max(1e-14, cutoff_);
    int n_aux_rank = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n_aux_; ++i) {
        if (evals(i) >= eig_tol) n_aux_rank++;
    }

    Eigen::MatrixXd U_kept(n_aux_, n_aux_rank);
    Eigen::VectorXd inv_sqrt_evals(n_aux_rank);
    int col_idx = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n_aux_; ++i) {
        if (evals(i) >= eig_tol) {
            U_kept.col(col_idx) = evecs.col(i);
            inv_sqrt_evals(col_idx) = 1.0 / std::sqrt(evals(i));
            col_idx++;
        }
    }

    // Matriks simetris J^{-1/2} = U * Lambda^{-1/2} * U^T
    Eigen::MatrixXd J_inv_sqrt = U_kept * inv_sqrt_evals.asDiagonal() * U_kept.transpose();

    int n_pairs = n_primary_ * n_primary_;
    Eigen::MatrixXd V_raw = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(n_pairs, n_aux_);

    // =====================================================================
    // 4. EVALUASI INTEGRAL 3-PUSAT (TANPA PERMUTASI PIVOT)
    // =====================================================================
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        std::vector<double> buffer(10000);
        #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1) nowait
        for (int i = 0; i < n_shells_prim; ++i) {
            int bf_i_start = prim_starts[i];
            int dim_i = prim_sizes[i];

            for (int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
                int bf_j_start = prim_starts[j];
                int dim_j = prim_sizes[j];

                for (int R = 0; R < n_shells_aux; ++R) {
                    int abs_R = n_shells_prim + R;
                    int dim_R = aux_sizes[R];
                    int aux_start = aux_starts[R];

                    if (!integrals_->compute_3c2e_block_raw(i, j, abs_R, buffer.data())) continue;

                    for (int r = 0; r < dim_R; ++r) {
                        int P = aux_start + r;

                        for (int p_j = 0; p_j < dim_j; ++p_j) {
                            int base_r1 = bf_j_start + p_j;
                            int base_r2 = (bf_j_start + p_j) * n_primary_;
                            int buffer_offset = dim_i * (p_j + dim_j * r);

                            for (int p_i = 0; p_i < dim_i; ++p_i) {
                                double val = buffer[p_i + buffer_offset];
                                int r1 = (bf_i_start + p_i) * n_primary_ + base_r1;
                                int r2 = base_r2 + (bf_i_start + p_i);

                                V_raw(r1, P) = val;
                                if (r1 != r2) V_raw(r2, P) = val;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // =====================================================================
    // 5. KONTRAKSI BLAS-3 DGEMM: B = (mn|P) * J^{-1/2}_{PQ}
    // =====================================================================
    B_mat_.noalias() = V_raw * J_inv_sqrt;

    if (save_hdf5) {
        utils::HDF5TensorIO io("df_tensor.h5", utils::HDF5TensorIO::Mode::WRITE_TRUNCATE);
        std::array<long, 4> dims = {(long)n_aux_, (long)n_primary_, (long)n_primary_, 1};
        std::array<long, 4> chunks = {1, (long)n_primary_, (long)n_primary_, 1};
        io.create_dataset_4d("df_tensor", dims, chunks);

        for (int P = 0; P < n_aux_; ++P) {
            std::array<long, 4> offset = {P, 0, 0, 0};
            io.write_slice_4d("df_tensor", offset, chunks, B_mat_.col(P).data());
        }
    }

    is_computed_ = true;
}

} 
}
