/**
 * @file src/mp2/mp2.cc
 * @brief Implementasi Terpadu BaseMP2, RMP2, dan UMP2
 */

#include <tblis/tblis.h>
#include "mshqc/symmetry/salc_builder.h"
#include "mshqc/mp2/mp2.h"
#include "mshqc/integrals/eri_transformer.h"
#include "mshqc/integrals/df_eri.h"
#include "mshqc/integrals/cholesky_eri.h"
#include "mshqc/gradient/optimizer.h"
#include <unsupported/Eigen/MatrixFunctions>
#include <iostream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <cmath>
#include <chrono>
#include <omp.h>

namespace mshqc {
using integrals::ERITransformer;

BaseMP2::BaseMP2(const Molecule& mol, const BasisSet& basis, 
                 std::shared_ptr<IntegralEngine> integrals, 
                 const SCFResult& scf_guess,
                 const MP2Config& config,
                 std::shared_ptr<PointGroup> pg,
                 std::shared_ptr<PetiteList> pl)
    : mol_(mol), basis_(basis), integrals_(integrals), scf_(scf_guess),
      config_(config), pg_(pg), pl_(pl) 
{
    nbf_ = static_cast<int>(scf_.C_alpha.rows());
    nocc_a_ = scf_.n_occ_alpha;
    nocc_b_ = scf_.n_occ_beta;
    nvir_a_ = nbf_ - nocc_a_;
    nvir_b_ = nbf_ - nocc_b_;

    if (config_.eri_method == "df") {
        config_.use_df = true;
    } else if (!config_.use_df && config_.eri_method != "cholesky") {
        config_.eri_method = "exact"; 
    }

    // Auto-build L_mat jika scf_ belum memiliki faktor 3-indeks yang sesuai
    if (config_.use_df && scf_.L_mat.rows() != nbf_ * nbf_) {
        std::string aux_name = config_.aux_basis_name.empty() ? (basis_.name() + "-RI") : config_.aux_basis_name;
        BasisSet aux_basis(aux_name, mol_);
        BasisSet combined = basis_;
        combined.append(aux_basis);
        auto df_ints = std::make_shared<IntegralEngine>(mol_, combined);
        integrals::DensityFittingERI df_eng(basis_, aux_basis, df_ints, config_.df_threshold);
        df_eng.compute(false);
        scf_.L_mat = df_eng.get_B_mat();
    } else if (config_.eri_method == "cholesky" && !config_.use_df && scf_.L_mat.rows() != nbf_ * nbf_) {
        integrals::CholeskyERI chol_eng(basis_, integrals_);
        chol_eng.set_threshold(config_.cholesky_threshold);
        chol_eng.compute();
        scf_.L_mat = chol_eng.get_L_mat();
    }
}
BaseMP2::~BaseMP2() = default;

MP2Result BaseMP2::compute() {
    return MP2Result();
}
void BaseMP2::transform_3center_mo() {
    int n_aux = scf_.L_mat.cols();
    bool is_restricted = (nocc_a_ == nocc_b_ && nvir_a_ == nvir_b_ && scf_.n_occ_alpha == scf_.n_occ_beta);
    B_ia_P_alpha_ = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nocc_a_ * nvir_a_, n_aux);

    const Eigen::MatrixXd& Ca_occ = scf_.C_alpha.leftCols(nocc_a_);
    const Eigen::MatrixXd& Ca_vir = scf_.C_alpha.rightCols(nvir_a_);
    Eigen::Map<const Eigen::MatrixXd> L_flat(scf_.L_mat.data(), nbf_, nbf_ * n_aux);

    Eigen::MatrixXd X_a = Ca_vir.transpose() * L_flat; 
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (int P = 0; P < n_aux; ++P) {
        Eigen::Map<Eigen::MatrixXd> X_P(X_a.data() + P * nvir_a_ * nbf_, nvir_a_, nbf_);
        Eigen::MatrixXd B_MO_a = X_P * Ca_occ; 
        
        for (int i = 0; i < nocc_a_; ++i) {
            for (int a = 0; a < nvir_a_; ++a) {
                B_ia_P_alpha_(i * nvir_a_ + a, P) = B_MO_a(a, i);
            }
        }
    }

    if (!is_restricted && nocc_b_ > 0 && nvir_b_ > 0) {
        B_ia_P_beta_ = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nocc_b_ * nvir_b_, n_aux);
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Cb_occ = scf_.C_beta.leftCols(nocc_b_);
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Cb_vir = scf_.C_beta.rightCols(nvir_b_);
        Eigen::MatrixXd X_b = Cb_vir.transpose() * L_flat;

        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
        for (int P = 0; P < n_aux; ++P) {
            Eigen::Map<Eigen::MatrixXd> X_P_b(X_b.data() + P * nvir_b_ * nbf_, nvir_b_, nbf_);
            Eigen::MatrixXd B_MO_b = X_P_b * Cb_occ;

            for (int i = 0; i < nocc_b_; ++i) {
                for (int a = 0; a < nvir_b_; ++a) {
                    B_ia_P_beta_(i * nvir_b_ + a, P) = B_MO_b(a, i);
                }
            }
        }
    }

}


namespace foundation {

void RMP2::transform_integrals() {
    if (config_.eri_method == "exact") {
        if (config_.print_level > 0) std::cout << "  [RMP2] Transformasi Exact OOVV (O(N^5))...\n";
        const auto& eri_ao = integrals_->compute_eri();
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& C_occ = scf_.C_alpha.leftCols(nocc_a_);
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& C_virt = scf_.C_alpha.rightCols(nvir_a_);
        auto eri_chemist = integrals::ERITransformer::transform_custom(
            eri_ao, C_occ, C_virt, C_occ, C_virt, nbf_, nocc_a_, nvir_a_, nocc_a_, nvir_a_
        );
        Eigen::array< int, 4 > shuffle_idxs = {0, 2, 1, 3}; 
        eri_mo_ = eri_chemist.shuffle(shuffle_idxs);
    } else {
        transform_3center_mo();
    }
}
void RMP2::compute_amplitudes_and_energy() {
    const Eigen::VectorXd& eps = scf_.orbital_energies_alpha;
    t2_ = Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>(nocc_a_, nocc_a_, nvir_a_, nvir_a_);
    t2_.setZero();

    double e_corr_sum = 0.0;
    bool is_exact = (config_.eri_method == "exact");

    // TAHAP OPTIMASI: GIANT DGEMM PENGGANTI LOOP .DOT()
    Eigen::MatrixXd G_aa;
    if (!is_exact) {
        G_aa = B_ia_P_alpha_ * B_ia_P_alpha_.transpose();
    }

    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) reduction(+:e_corr_sum) schedule(static)
    for (int i = 0; i < nocc_a_; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j < nocc_a_; ++j) {
            if (i < n_frozen_ || j < n_frozen_) continue;
            double e_ij = eps(i) + eps(j);

            for (int a = 0; a < nvir_a_; ++a) {
                double den_a = e_ij - eps(nocc_a_ + a);
                int idx_ia = (!is_exact) ? (i * nvir_a_ + a) : 0;
                int idx_ja = (!is_exact) ? (j * nvir_a_ + a) : 0;

                for (int b = 0; b < nvir_a_; ++b) {
                    double denom = den_a - eps(nocc_a_ + b);
                    if (std::abs(denom) < 1e-12) {
                        t2_(i, j, a, b) = 0.0; 
                        continue;
                    }

                    double val_iajb = 0.0, val_ibja = 0.0;

                    if (is_exact) {
                        val_iajb = eri_mo_(i, j, a, b);
                        val_ibja = eri_mo_(i, j, b, a);
                    } else {
                        int idx_jb = j * nvir_a_ + b;
                        int idx_ib = i * nvir_a_ + b;
                        
                        // O(1) Memory Access menggantikan O(N) .dot() loop
                        val_iajb = G_aa(idx_ia, idx_jb);
                        val_ibja = G_aa(idx_ib, idx_ja);
                    }

                    double t_val = val_iajb / denom;
                    t2_(i, j, a, b) = t_val;
                    e_corr_sum += t_val * (2.0 * val_iajb - val_ibja);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    e_corr_ = e_corr_sum;
}

MP2Result RMP2::compute() {
    auto t1 = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    transform_integrals();
    compute_amplitudes_and_energy();

    auto t2 = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    MP2Result result;
    result.energy_scf = scf_.energy_total;
    result.energy_mp2_corr = e_corr_;
    result.energy_total = scf_.energy_total + e_corr_;
    result.t2_aa = t2_; 
    result.B_ia_P_alpha = B_ia_P_alpha_;

    if (config_.print_level > 0) {
        std::cout << "\n=== RMP2 Results (" << config_.eri_method << ") ===\n";
        std::cout << std::fixed << std::setprecision(8);
        std::cout << "RHF Energy:      " << std::setw(14) << result.energy_scf << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "MP2 Correlation: " << std::setw(14) << result.energy_mp2_corr << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "Total RMP2:      " << std::setw(14) << result.energy_total << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "Time:            " << std::chrono::duration<double>(t2-t1).count() << " s\n";
    }
    return result;
}

} // namespace foundation

void UMP2::transform_integrals() {
    if (config_.eri_method != "exact") {
        transform_3center_mo();
    }
}

double UMP2::compute_ss_alpha() {
    bool is_exact = (config_.eri_method == "exact");
    if (is_exact) {
        const auto& eri_ao = integrals_->compute_eri();
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Ca_occ = scf_.C_alpha.leftCols(nocc_a_);
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Ca_vir = scf_.C_alpha.rightCols(nvir_a_);
        auto eri_chem = integrals::ERITransformer::transform_custom(
            eri_ao, Ca_occ, Ca_vir, Ca_occ, Ca_vir, nbf_, nocc_a_, nvir_a_, nocc_a_, nvir_a_
        );
        Eigen::array< int, 4 > shuf = {0, 2, 1, 3};
        eri_aaaa_ = eri_chem.shuffle(shuf);
    }

    t2_aa_ = Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>(nocc_a_, nocc_a_, nvir_a_, nvir_a_);
    t2_aa_.setZero();
    const auto& eo = scf_.orbital_energies_alpha;
    const auto& ev = scf_.orbital_energies_alpha.tail(nvir_a_);
    double e_sum = 0.0;

    Eigen::MatrixXd G_aa;
    if (!is_exact) G_aa = B_ia_P_alpha_ * B_ia_P_alpha_.transpose();

    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(3) reduction(+:e_sum) schedule(static)
    for(int b = 0; b < nvir_a_; ++b) {
        for(int a = 0; a < nvir_a_; ++a) {
            for(int j = 0; j < nocc_a_; ++j) {
                if (j < n_frozen_) continue;
                double den_partial = -ev(a) - ev(b) + eo(j);

                for(int i = 0; i < nocc_a_; ++i) {
                    if (i < n_frozen_) continue;
                    double den = den_partial + eo(i);
                    double val_iajb = 0.0, val_ibja = 0.0;
                    
                    if (is_exact) {
                        val_iajb = eri_aaaa_(i, j, a, b);
                        val_ibja = eri_aaaa_(i, j, b, a);
                    } else {
                        val_iajb = G_aa(i * nvir_a_ + a, j * nvir_a_ + b);
                        val_ibja = G_aa(i * nvir_a_ + b, j * nvir_a_ + a);
                    }

                    double val_num = val_iajb - val_ibja;
                    double safe_den = (std::abs(den) < config_.level_shift) 
                                    ? std::copysign(config_.level_shift, den) : den;
                   
                    double val_t = val_num / safe_den;
                    t2_aa_(i, j, a, b) = val_t;
                    e_sum += val_t * val_num;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if (is_exact) eri_aaaa_.resize(0,0,0,0);
    return 0.25 * e_sum;
}

double UMP2::compute_ss_beta() {
    if (nocc_b_ == 0 || nvir_b_ == 0) return 0.0;
    bool is_exact = (config_.eri_method == "exact");
    if (is_exact) {
        const auto& eri_ao = integrals_->compute_eri();
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Cb_occ = scf_.C_beta.leftCols(nocc_b_);
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Cb_vir = scf_.C_beta.rightCols(nvir_b_);
        auto eri_chem = integrals::ERITransformer::transform_custom(
            eri_ao, Cb_occ, Cb_vir, Cb_occ, Cb_vir, nbf_, nocc_b_, nvir_b_, nocc_b_, nvir_b_
        );
        Eigen::array< int, 4 > shuf = {0, 2, 1, 3};
        eri_bbbb_ = eri_chem.shuffle(shuf);
    }

    t2_bb_ = Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>(nocc_b_, nocc_b_, nvir_b_, nvir_b_);
    t2_bb_.setZero();
    const auto& eo = scf_.orbital_energies_beta;
    const auto& ev = scf_.orbital_energies_beta.tail(nvir_b_);
    double e_sum = 0.0;

    // GIANT DGEMM: Hitung seluruh integral (ia|jb)_beta sekaligus dengan BLAS-3
    Eigen::MatrixXd G_bb;
    if (!is_exact) G_bb = B_ia_P_beta_ * B_ia_P_beta_.transpose();

    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(3) reduction(+:e_sum) schedule(static)
    for(int b = 0; b < nvir_b_; ++b) {
        for(int a = 0; a < nvir_b_; ++a) {
            for(int j = 0; j < nocc_b_; ++j) {
                if (j < n_frozen_) continue;
                double den_partial = -ev(a) - ev(b) + eo(j);

                for(int i = 0; i < nocc_b_; ++i) {
                    if (i < n_frozen_) continue;
                    double den = den_partial + eo(i);
                    if (std::abs(den) < 1e-12) { t2_bb_(i, j, a, b) = 0.0; continue; }

                    double val_iajb = 0.0, val_ibja = 0.0;
                    if (is_exact) {
                        val_iajb = eri_bbbb_(i, j, a, b);
                        val_ibja = eri_bbbb_(i, j, b, a);
                    } else {
                        val_iajb = G_bb(i * nvir_b_ + a, j * nvir_b_ + b);
                        val_ibja = G_bb(i * nvir_b_ + b, j * nvir_b_ + a);
                    }

                    double val_num = val_iajb - val_ibja;
                    double val_t = val_num / den;
                    t2_bb_(i, j, a, b) = val_t;
                    e_sum += val_t * val_num;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if (is_exact) eri_bbbb_.resize(0,0,0,0);
    return 0.25 * e_sum;
}

double UMP2::compute_os() {
    if (nocc_a_ == 0 || nvir_a_ == 0 || nocc_b_ == 0 || nvir_b_ == 0) return 0.0;
    bool is_exact = (config_.eri_method == "exact");
    if (is_exact) {
        const auto& eri_ao = integrals_->compute_eri();
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Ca_occ = scf_.C_alpha.leftCols(nocc_a_);
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Ca_vir = scf_.C_alpha.rightCols(nvir_a_);
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Cb_occ = scf_.C_beta.leftCols(nocc_b_);
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Cb_vir = scf_.C_beta.rightCols(nvir_b_);
        auto eri_chem = integrals::ERITransformer::transform_custom(
            eri_ao, Ca_occ, Ca_vir, Cb_occ, Cb_vir, nbf_, nocc_a_, nvir_a_, nocc_b_, nvir_b_
        );
        Eigen::array< int, 4 > shuf = {0, 2, 1, 3};
        eri_aabb_ = eri_chem.shuffle(shuf);
    }

    t2_ab_ = Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>(nocc_a_, nocc_b_, nvir_a_, nvir_b_);
    t2_ab_.setZero();
    const auto& eoa = scf_.orbital_energies_alpha;
    const auto& eva = scf_.orbital_energies_alpha.tail(nvir_a_);
    const auto& eob = scf_.orbital_energies_beta;
    const auto& evb = scf_.orbital_energies_beta.tail(nvir_b_);
    double e_sum = 0.0;

    // GIANT DGEMM: Hitung seluruh integral (ia|jb)_alpha_beta sekaligus dengan BLAS-3
    Eigen::MatrixXd G_ab;
    if (!is_exact) G_ab = B_ia_P_alpha_ * B_ia_P_beta_.transpose();

    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(3) reduction(+:e_sum) schedule(static)
    for(int b = 0; b < nvir_b_; ++b) {
        for(int a = 0; a < nvir_a_; ++a) {
            for(int j = 0; j < nocc_b_; ++j) {
                if (j < n_frozen_) continue;
                double den_partial = -eva(a) - evb(b) + eob(j);

                for(int i = 0; i < nocc_a_; ++i) {
                    if (i < n_frozen_) continue;
                    double den = den_partial + eoa(i);
                    if (std::abs(den) < 1e-12) { t2_ab_(i, j, a, b) = 0.0; continue; }

                    double val_num = is_exact ? eri_aabb_(i, j, a, b) 
                                              : G_ab(i * nvir_a_ + a, j * nvir_b_ + b);

                    double val_t = val_num / den;
                    t2_ab_(i, j, a, b) = val_t;
                    e_sum += val_t * val_num;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if (is_exact) eri_aabb_.resize(0,0,0,0);
    return e_sum;
}

MP2Result UMP2::compute() {
    auto start_time = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();



    transform_integrals();
    double e_ss_aa = compute_ss_alpha();
    double e_ss_bb = compute_ss_beta();
    double e_os    = compute_os();
    double e_corr  = e_ss_aa + e_ss_bb + e_os;

    auto end_time = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    MP2Result result;
    result.energy_scf = scf_.energy_total;
    result.energy_mp2_ss = e_ss_aa + e_ss_bb;
    result.energy_mp2_os = e_os;
    result.energy_mp2_corr = e_corr;
    result.energy_total = scf_.energy_total + e_corr;
    result.t2_aa = t2_aa_;
    result.t2_bb = t2_bb_;
    result.t2_ab = t2_ab_;
    result.B_ia_P_alpha = B_ia_P_alpha_; 
    result.B_ia_P_beta  = B_ia_P_beta_;  
    if(config_.print_level > 0) {
        std::cout << "\n=== UMP2 Results (" << config_.eri_method << ") ===\n";
        std::cout << std::fixed << std::setprecision(8);
        std::cout << "SS Energy (AA):  " << std::setw(14) << e_ss_aa << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "SS Energy (BB):  " << std::setw(14) << e_ss_bb << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "OS Energy (AB):  " << std::setw(14) << e_os << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "Correlation E:   " << std::setw(14) << e_corr << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "Total Energy:    " << std::setw(14) << result.energy_total << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "Time:            " << std::chrono::duration<double>(end_time - start_time).count() << " s\n";
    }
    return result;
}

T2Amplitudes UMP2::get_t2_amplitudes() const { 
    T2Amplitudes amps;
    amps.t2_aa = t2_aa_;
    amps.t2_bb = t2_bb_;
    amps.t2_ab = t2_ab_;
    return amps;
}
void ROMP2::semicanonicalize() {
    // Pastikan matriks Fock AO fisik tersedia; jika belum ada, bangun dari matriks densitas ROHF
    if (scf_.F_alpha.rows() != nbf_ || scf_.F_beta.rows() != nbf_) {
        throw std::runtime_error("ROMP2 Error: F_alpha / F_beta AO matrices are missing in SCFResult.");
    }

    auto diag_subspaces = [&](const Eigen::MatrixXd& F_ao, Eigen::MatrixXd& C,
                              Eigen::VectorXd& eps, int nocc, int nvir) {
        if (nocc <= 0 || nvir <= 0) return;
        Eigen::MatrixXd C_occ = C.leftCols(nocc);
        Eigen::MatrixXd C_vir = C.rightCols(nvir);

        Eigen::MatrixXd F_oo = C_occ.transpose() * F_ao * C_occ;
        Eigen::MatrixXd F_vv = C_vir.transpose() * F_ao * C_vir;

        Eigen::SelfAdjointEigenSolver<Eigen::MatrixXd> es_o(0.5 * (F_oo + F_oo.transpose()));
        Eigen::SelfAdjointEigenSolver<Eigen::MatrixXd> es_v(0.5 * (F_vv + F_vv.transpose()));

        C.leftCols(nocc).noalias()  = C_occ * es_o.eigenvectors();
        C.rightCols(nvir).noalias() = C_vir * es_v.eigenvectors();

        eps.resize(nocc + nvir);
        eps.head(nocc) = es_o.eigenvalues();
        eps.tail(nvir) = es_v.eigenvalues();
    };

    // Semikanonikalisasi kanal spin Alpha (DOCC + SOCC vs VIR)
    diag_subspaces(scf_.F_alpha, scf_.C_alpha, scf_.orbital_energies_alpha, nocc_a_, nvir_a_);
    scf_.P_alpha.noalias() = scf_.C_alpha.leftCols(nocc_a_) * scf_.C_alpha.leftCols(nocc_a_).transpose();

    // Semikanonikalisasi kanal spin Beta (DOCC vs SOCC + VIR)
    if (nocc_b_ > 0 && nvir_b_ > 0) {
        diag_subspaces(scf_.F_beta, scf_.C_beta, scf_.orbital_energies_beta, nocc_b_, nvir_b_);
        scf_.P_beta.noalias() = scf_.C_beta.leftCols(nocc_b_) * scf_.C_beta.leftCols(nocc_b_).transpose();
    }
}

double ROMP2::compute_singles_energy() const {
    double e_singles = 0.0;

    if (nocc_a_ > 0 && nvir_a_ > 0) {
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Ca_occ = scf_.C_alpha.leftCols(nocc_a_);
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Ca_vir = scf_.C_alpha.rightCols(nvir_a_);
        Eigen::MatrixXd F_ov_a = Ca_occ.transpose() * scf_.F_alpha * Ca_vir;
        const auto& eps_a = scf_.orbital_energies_alpha;

        for (int i = n_frozen_; i < nocc_a_; ++i) {
            for (int a = 0; a < nvir_a_; ++a) {
                double denom = eps_a(i) - eps_a(nocc_a_ + a);
                if (std::abs(denom) > 1e-12) {
                    e_singles += (F_ov_a(i, a) * F_ov_a(i, a)) / denom;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (nocc_b_ > 0 && nvir_b_ > 0) {
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Cb_occ = scf_.C_beta.leftCols(nocc_b_);
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Cb_vir = scf_.C_beta.rightCols(nvir_b_);
        Eigen::MatrixXd F_ov_b = Cb_occ.transpose() * scf_.F_beta * Cb_vir;
        const auto& eps_b = scf_.orbital_energies_beta;

        for (int i = n_frozen_; i < nocc_b_; ++i) {
            for (int a = 0; a < nvir_b_; ++a) {
                double denom = eps_b(i) - eps_b(nocc_b_ + a);
                if (std::abs(denom) > 1e-12) {
                    e_singles += (F_ov_b(i, a) * F_ov_b(i, a)) / denom;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    return e_singles;
}

MP2Result ROMP2::compute() {
    auto start_time = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    // 1. Rotasi satu langkah ke basis semikanonik untuk menghapus artefak level-shift ROHF
    semicanonicalize();

    // 2. Transformasi integral 3-indeks (DF/Cholesky) atau persiapan Exact
    transform_integrals();
    if (config_.eri_method != "exact" && nocc_a_ == nocc_b_ && B_ia_P_beta_.rows() == 0) {
        B_ia_P_beta_ = B_ia_P_alpha_;
    }

    // 3. Evaluasi energi Singles (non-Brillouin) dan Doubles (AA, BB, AB)
    double e_singles = compute_singles_energy();
    double e_ss_aa   = compute_ss_alpha();
    double e_ss_bb   = compute_ss_beta();
    double e_os      = compute_os();
    double e_corr    = e_singles + e_ss_aa + e_ss_bb + e_os;

    auto end_time = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    MP2Result result;
    result.converged               = true;
    result.iterations              = 1;
    result.energy_scf              = scf_.energy_total;
    result.energy_mp2_singles      = e_singles;
    result.energy_mp2_ss           = e_ss_aa + e_ss_bb;
    result.energy_mp2_os           = e_os;
    result.energy_mp2_corr         = e_corr;
    result.energy_total            = scf_.energy_total + e_corr;
    result.t2_aa                   = t2_aa_;
    result.t2_bb                   = t2_bb_;
    result.t2_ab                   = t2_ab_;
    result.B_ia_P_alpha            = B_ia_P_alpha_;
    result.B_ia_P_beta             = B_ia_P_beta_;
    result.C_alpha                 = scf_.C_alpha;
    result.C_beta                  = scf_.C_beta;
    result.orbital_energies_alpha  = scf_.orbital_energies_alpha;
    result.orbital_energies_beta   = scf_.orbital_energies_beta;
    result.n_occ_alpha  = nocc_a_;
    result.n_occ_beta   = nocc_b_;
    result.n_virt_alpha = nvir_a_;
    result.n_virt_beta  = nvir_b_;

    if (config_.print_level > 0 && omp_get_thread_num() == 0) {
        std::cout << "\n=== ROMP2 Results (" << config_.eri_method << ") ===\n";
        std::cout << std::fixed << std::setprecision(8);
        std::cout << "ROHF Energy:     " << std::setw(14) << result.energy_scf << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "Singles Energy:  " << std::setw(14) << e_singles << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "SS Energy (AA):  " << std::setw(14) << e_ss_aa << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "SS Energy (BB):  " << std::setw(14) << e_ss_bb << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "OS Energy (AB):  " << std::setw(14) << e_os << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "Correlation E:   " << std::setw(14) << e_corr << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "Total ROMP2:     " << std::setw(14) << result.energy_total << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "Time:            " << std::chrono::duration<double>(end_time - start_time).count() << " s\n";
    }
    return result;
}

OMP2::OMP2(const Molecule& mol, const BasisSet& basis, 
           std::shared_ptr<IntegralEngine> integrals, 
           const SCFResult& scf_guess,
           const MP2Config& config,         
           std::shared_ptr<PointGroup> pg,
           std::shared_ptr<PetiteList> pl)  
    : BaseMP2(mol, basis, integrals, scf_guess, config, pg, pl)
{
    na_  = nocc_a_; 
    nb_  = nocc_b_;
    va_  = nvir_a_; 
    vb_  = nvir_b_;
    n_frozen_ = 0; 

    e_ss_ = 0.0;
    e_os_ = 0.0;

    max_iter_ = config_.max_iterations; 
    conv_thresh_ = config_.energy_threshold; 
   if (config_.gradient_threshold > 0.0) {
        grad_thresh_ = config_.gradient_threshold; 
    } else {
        grad_thresh_ = std::sqrt(config_.energy_threshold); 
    }

    symmetrizer_ = nullptr;
    init_fast_integrals();
}


void OMP2::init_fast_integrals() {
    S_ = integrals_->compute_overlap();
    H_core_ = integrals_->compute_core_hamiltonian();
    if (config_.eri_method == "exact") {
        integrals_->compute_eri();
    }
}

void OMP2::transform_integrals() {
    bool is_restricted = (na_ == nb_ && va_ == vb_ && mol_.multiplicity() == 1);
    scf_.irreps_alpha.assign(nbf_, 0);
    if (!is_restricted && nb_ > 0) {
        scf_.irreps_beta.assign(nbf_, 0);
    }
    if (config_.eri_method == "exact") {
        const auto& eri_ao = integrals_->compute_eri();
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Ca_o = scf_.C_alpha.leftCols(na_);
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& Ca_v = scf_.C_alpha.rightCols(va_);

        g_aa_.clear(); g_bb_.clear(); g_ab_.clear();

        if (!is_restricted && nb_ > 0 && vb_ > 0) {
            const Eigen::MatrixXd& Cb_o = scf_.C_beta.leftCols(nb_);
            const Eigen::MatrixXd& Cb_v = scf_.C_beta.rightCols(vb_);

            auto half_ov_a = integrals::ERITransformer::transform_half_bra(eri_ao, Ca_o, Ca_v, nbf_, na_, va_);
            g_aa_.allocate_block(0, 0, 0, 0, na_, va_, na_, va_);
            *(g_aa_.get_block(0, 0, 0, 0)) = integrals::ERITransformer::transform_finish_ket(half_ov_a, Ca_o, Ca_v, nbf_, na_, va_, na_, va_);

            g_ab_.allocate_block(0, 0, 0, 0, na_, va_, nb_, vb_);
            *(g_ab_.get_block(0, 0, 0, 0)) = integrals::ERITransformer::transform_finish_ket(half_ov_a, Cb_o, Cb_v, nbf_, na_, va_, nb_, vb_);
            half_ov_a.resize(0, 0, 0, 0);

            g_bb_.allocate_block(0, 0, 0, 0, nb_, vb_, nb_, vb_);
            *(g_bb_.get_block(0, 0, 0, 0)) = integrals::ERITransformer::transform_custom(eri_ao, Cb_o, Cb_v, Cb_o, Cb_v, nbf_, nb_, vb_, nb_, vb_);
        } else {
            g_aa_.allocate_block(0, 0, 0, 0, na_, va_, na_, va_);
            *(g_aa_.get_block(0, 0, 0, 0)) = integrals::ERITransformer::transform_custom(eri_ao, Ca_o, Ca_v, Ca_o, Ca_v, nbf_, na_, va_, na_, va_);
        }
    } else {
        scf_.C_alpha = C_a_current_;
        if (!is_restricted) scf_.C_beta = C_b_current_;
        
        transform_3center_mo(); 

        g_aa_.clear(); g_bb_.clear(); g_ab_.clear();

        g_aa_.allocate_block(0, 0, 0, 0, na_, va_, na_, va_);
        auto* g_blk = g_aa_.get_block(0, 0, 0, 0);

        if (g_blk) {
            Eigen::MatrixXd G_tmp_aa = B_ia_P_alpha_ * B_ia_P_alpha_.transpose();
            #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
            for (int j = 0; j < na_; ++j) {
                for (int b = 0; b < va_; ++b) {
                    int idx_jb = j * va_ + b;
                    for (int i = 0; i < na_; ++i) {
                        for (int a = 0; a < va_; ++a) {
                            int idx_ia = i * va_ + a;
                            (*g_blk)(i, a, j, b) = G_tmp_aa(idx_ia, idx_jb);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        
        if (!is_restricted && nb_ > 0 && vb_ > 0) {
            g_bb_.allocate_block(0, 0, 0, 0, nb_, vb_, nb_, vb_);
            auto* ptr_bb = g_bb_.get_block(0, 0, 0, 0);

            g_ab_.allocate_block(0, 0, 0, 0, na_, va_, nb_, vb_);
            auto* ptr_ab = g_ab_.get_block(0, 0, 0, 0);

            Eigen::MatrixXd G_tmp_bb = B_ia_P_beta_ * B_ia_P_beta_.transpose();
            Eigen::MatrixXd G_tmp_ab = B_ia_P_alpha_ * B_ia_P_beta_.transpose();

            #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
            for (int j = 0; j < nb_; ++j) {
                for (int b = 0; b < vb_; ++b) {
                    int idx_jb = j * vb_ + b;
                    for (int i = 0; i < nb_; ++i) {
                        for (int a = 0; a < vb_; ++a) {
                            int idx_ia = i * vb_ + a;
                            (*ptr_bb)(i, a, j, b) = G_tmp_bb(idx_ia, idx_jb);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
            for (int j = 0; j < nb_; ++j) {
                for (int b = 0; b < vb_; ++b) {
                    int idx_jb = j * vb_ + b;
                    for (int i = 0; i < na_; ++i) {
                        for (int a = 0; a < va_; ++a) {
                            int idx_ia = i * va_ + a;
                            (*ptr_ab)(i, a, j, b) = G_tmp_ab(idx_ia, idx_jb);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
void OMP2::pseudocanonicalize() {
    build_fock_fast(scf_.P_alpha, scf_.P_beta, scf_.F_alpha, scf_.F_beta);
    
    auto diag_block = [&](const Eigen::MatrixXd& F_ao, Eigen::MatrixXd& C, Eigen::VectorXd& eps, int nocc, int nvir) {
        Eigen::MatrixXd C_occ = C.leftCols(nocc);
        Eigen::MatrixXd C_vir = C.rightCols(nvir);
        Eigen::MatrixXd F_oo(nocc, nocc);
        Eigen::MatrixXd F_vv(nvir, nvir);
        F_oo.noalias() = C_occ.transpose() * (F_ao * C_occ);
        F_vv.noalias() = C_vir.transpose() * (F_ao * C_vir);

        Eigen::SelfAdjointEigenSolver<Eigen::MatrixXd> es_o(F_oo);
        Eigen::SelfAdjointEigenSolver<Eigen::MatrixXd> es_v(F_vv);
        C.leftCols(nocc).noalias() = C_occ * es_o.eigenvectors();
        C.rightCols(nvir).noalias() = C_vir * es_v.eigenvectors();

        eps.resize(nocc + nvir);
        eps.head(nocc) = es_o.eigenvalues();
        eps.tail(nvir) = es_v.eigenvalues();
    };

    diag_block(scf_.F_alpha, scf_.C_alpha, scf_.orbital_energies_alpha, na_, va_);
    scf_.P_alpha.noalias() = scf_.C_alpha.leftCols(na_) * scf_.C_alpha.leftCols(na_).transpose();

    if (nb_ > 0 && vb_ > 0) {
        diag_block(scf_.F_beta, scf_.C_beta, scf_.orbital_energies_beta, nb_, vb_);
        scf_.P_beta.noalias() = scf_.C_beta.leftCols(nb_) * scf_.C_beta.leftCols(nb_).transpose();
    }
}

void OMP2::transform_3center_mo_cholesky() {
    int n_chol = scf_.L_mat.cols();
    B_ia_P_alpha_ = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(na_ * va_, n_chol);
    B_oo_P_alpha_ = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(na_ * na_, n_chol);
    B_vv_P_alpha_ = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(va_ * va_, n_chol);
    
    bool is_restricted = (na_ == nb_ && va_ == vb_ && mol_.multiplicity() == 1);
    bool has_beta = (!is_restricted && nb_ > 0 && vb_ > 0);
    
    if (has_beta) {
        B_ia_P_beta_ = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nb_ * vb_, n_chol);
        B_oo_P_beta_ = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nb_ * nb_, n_chol);
        B_vv_P_beta_ = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(vb_ * vb_, n_chol);
    }

    const Eigen::MatrixXd& Ca_occ = scf_.C_alpha.leftCols(na_);
    const Eigen::MatrixXd& Ca_vir = scf_.C_alpha.rightCols(va_);
    Eigen::MatrixXd Cb_occ, Cb_vir;
    if (has_beta) {
        Cb_occ = scf_.C_beta.leftCols(nb_);
        Cb_vir = scf_.C_beta.rightCols(vb_);
    }

    int chunk_size = 128; 

    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic)
    for (int P_start = 0; P_start < n_chol; P_start += chunk_size) {
        int P_end = std::min(n_chol, P_start + chunk_size);
        int P_size = P_end - P_start;

        // Gunakan sintaks deklarasi Tipe lengkap
        Eigen::Map< const Eigen::MatrixXd > L_chunk(scf_.L_mat.col(P_start).data(), nbf_ * nbf_, P_size);
        Eigen::Map< const Eigen::MatrixXd > L_reshaped(L_chunk.data(), nbf_, nbf_ * P_size);

        Eigen::MatrixXd X_a_occ = Ca_occ.transpose() * L_reshaped;
        Eigen::MatrixXd X_a_vir = Ca_vir.transpose() * L_reshaped;

        for (int p = 0; p < P_size; ++p) {
            Eigen::Map< Eigen::MatrixXd > X_P_occ(X_a_occ.data() + p * na_ * nbf_, na_, nbf_);
            Eigen::Map< Eigen::MatrixXd > X_P_vir(X_a_vir.data() + p * va_ * nbf_, va_, nbf_);
            
            // Simpan perkalian matriks ke dalam sementara baru dipetakan ke .col()
            Eigen::MatrixXd B_oo_tmp = X_P_occ * Ca_occ;
            Eigen::MatrixXd B_ia_tmp = X_P_vir * Ca_occ;
            Eigen::MatrixXd B_vv_tmp = X_P_vir * Ca_vir;
            
            B_oo_P_alpha_.col(P_start + p) = Eigen::Map< Eigen::VectorXd >(B_oo_tmp.data(), na_ * na_);
            B_ia_P_alpha_.col(P_start + p) = Eigen::Map< Eigen::VectorXd >(B_ia_tmp.data(), va_ * na_);
            B_vv_P_alpha_.col(P_start + p) = Eigen::Map< Eigen::VectorXd >(B_vv_tmp.data(), va_ * va_);
        }

        if (has_beta) {
            Eigen::MatrixXd X_b_occ = Cb_occ.transpose() * L_reshaped;
            Eigen::MatrixXd X_b_vir = Cb_vir.transpose() * L_reshaped;

            for (int p = 0; p < P_size; ++p) {
                Eigen::Map< Eigen::MatrixXd > X_P_occ_b(X_b_occ.data() + p * nb_ * nbf_, nb_, nbf_);
                Eigen::Map< Eigen::MatrixXd > X_P_vir_b(X_b_vir.data() + p * vb_ * nbf_, vb_, nbf_);
                
                Eigen::MatrixXd B_oo_tmp_b = X_P_occ_b * Cb_occ;
                Eigen::MatrixXd B_ia_tmp_b = X_P_vir_b * Cb_occ;
                Eigen::MatrixXd B_vv_tmp_b = X_P_vir_b * Cb_vir;
                
                B_oo_P_beta_.col(P_start + p) = Eigen::Map< Eigen::VectorXd >(B_oo_tmp_b.data(), nb_ * nb_);
                B_ia_P_beta_.col(P_start + p) = Eigen::Map< Eigen::VectorXd >(B_ia_tmp_b.data(), vb_ * nb_);
                B_vv_P_beta_.col(P_start + p) = Eigen::Map< Eigen::VectorXd >(B_vv_tmp_b.data(), vb_ * vb_);
            }
        }
    }
    

}

double OMP2::execute_micro_iterations() {
    bool is_restricted = (na_ == nb_ && va_ == vb_ && mol_.multiplicity() == 1);
    double old_energy = e_ss_ + e_os_;
    scf_.C_alpha = C_a_current_;
    scf_.C_beta  = C_b_current_;
    scf_.P_alpha = scf_.C_alpha.leftCols(na_) * scf_.C_alpha.leftCols(na_).transpose();
    scf_.P_beta  = scf_.C_beta.leftCols(nb_)  * scf_.C_beta.leftCols(nb_).transpose();

    pseudocanonicalize();

    C_a_current_ = scf_.C_alpha;
    C_b_current_ = scf_.C_beta;

    if (config_.eri_method == "cholesky" || config_.eri_method == "df") {
        scf_.irreps_alpha.assign(nbf_, 0);
        if (!is_restricted && nb_ > 0) {
            scf_.irreps_beta.assign(nbf_, 0);
        }       
        
     
        transform_3center_mo_cholesky();
        compute_t2_and_energy_cholesky();
        
    } else {
        transform_integrals(); 
        compute_t2_amplitudes();
        compute_mp2_energy();
    }

    build_opdm_alpha();
    if (!is_restricted && nb_ > 0) {
        build_opdm_beta();
    } else if (is_restricted && nb_ > 0) {
        G_oo_beta_ = G_oo_alpha_; 
    }
    (void)old_energy;
    return get_correlation_energy();
}

void OMP2::execute_macro_iterations(DIIS& diis_a, DIIS& diis_b, int macro_iter) {
    build_generalized_fock();

    bool is_restricted = (na_ == nb_ && va_ == vb_ && mol_.multiplicity() == 1);
    

    if (is_restricted && nb_ > 0) F_gen_b_ = F_gen_a_; 

    int dim_a = va_ * na_;
    int dim_b = is_restricted ? 0 : (nb_ > 0 ? vb_ * nb_ : 0);
    int n_params = dim_a + dim_b;

    if (orbital_gradient_.size() != n_params) orbital_gradient_.resize(n_params);

    int idx = 0;
    bool use_sym = (!scf_.irreps_alpha.empty() && scf_.irreps_alpha[0] != -1);
    
    if (!is_restricted && nb_ > 0) {
    
        Eigen::MatrixXd wa = 2.0 * F_gen_a_.block(na_, 0, va_, na_);
        for (int i = 0; i < na_; ++i) {
            for (int a = 0; a < va_; ++a) {
                if (use_sym && (scf_.irreps_alpha[i] ^ scf_.irreps_alpha[na_ + a]) != 0) orbital_gradient_(idx++) = 0.0;
                else orbital_gradient_(idx++) = wa(a, i);
            }
        }
        Eigen::MatrixXd wb = 2.0 * F_gen_b_.block(nb_, 0, vb_, nb_);
        for (int i = 0; i < nb_; ++i) {
            for (int b = 0; b < vb_; ++b) {
                if (use_sym && (scf_.irreps_beta[i] ^ scf_.irreps_beta[nb_ + b]) != 0) orbital_gradient_(idx++) = 0.0;
                else orbital_gradient_(idx++) = wb(b, i);
            }
        }
    } else {
    
        Eigen::MatrixXd wa = 2.0 * F_gen_a_.block(na_, 0, va_, na_);
        Eigen::MatrixXd wb = 2.0 * F_gen_b_.block(nb_, 0, vb_, nb_);
        Eigen::MatrixXd w_sym = wa + wb;
        
        for (int i = 0; i < na_; ++i) {
            for (int a = 0; a < va_; ++a) {
                if (use_sym && (scf_.irreps_alpha[i] ^ scf_.irreps_alpha[na_ + a]) != 0) orbital_gradient_(idx++) = 0.0;
                else orbital_gradient_(idx++) = w_sym(a, i);
            }
        }
    }
}
MP2Result OMP2::compute() {
    C_a_current_ = scf_.C_alpha;
    C_b_current_ = scf_.C_beta;

    double e_total_best = 1e99;
    double e_corr_best = 0.0;
    double e_total_last = 1e99;
    double trust_radius = 0.15; 
    double expected_change = -1e-6;

    Eigen::MatrixXd C_a_last = scf_.C_alpha;
    Eigen::MatrixXd C_b_last = scf_.C_beta;

    DIIS diis_alpha(6);
    DIIS diis_beta(6);

    const bool is_omp2_instance = (typeid(*this) == typeid(OMP2));
    const char* method_tag = is_omp2_instance ? "OMP2" : "OMP3";

    if (omp_get_thread_num() == 0) {
        std::cout << "\n========================================================\n";
        if (is_omp2_instance) {
            std::cout << "      Orbital-Optimized MP2 (OMP2)\n";
        } else {
            std::cout << "      Orbital-Optimized MP3 (OMP3)\n";
        }
        std::cout << "      Trust-Region SOSCF (Approximate Hessian)\n";
        std::cout << "========================================================\n";
    }

    bool is_converged = false;
    int macro_iter = 0;
    bool is_restricted = (na_ == nb_ && va_ == vb_ && mol_.multiplicity() == 1);
    bool step_rejected = false;

    if (omp_get_thread_num() == 0 && config_.print_level >= 3 && na_ > 0 && va_ > 0) {
        std::cout << "\n--- [DEBUG] Memulai Uji Finite-Difference " << method_tag << " (Total Energy) ---\n";
        int i_target = na_ - 1; 
        int a_target = 0;
        
        execute_micro_iterations();
        build_generalized_fock();
        
        double grad_ana = is_restricted ? -4.0 * F_gen_a_(na_ + a_target, i_target) : 
                                          -2.0 * F_gen_a_(na_ + a_target, i_target);
                          
        double theta = 1e-5;
        Eigen::MatrixXd C_a_orig = scf_.C_alpha;
        Eigen::MatrixXd C_b_orig = scf_.C_beta;
        
        auto calc_tot = [&](double t) {
            Eigen::MatrixXd U = Eigen::MatrixXd::Identity(nbf_, nbf_);
            U(i_target, na_ + a_target) = t; U(na_ + a_target, i_target) = -t;
            
            C_a_current_ = C_a_orig * U;
            if (nb_ > 0) C_b_current_ = C_b_orig * U;
            
            double e_corr_t = execute_micro_iterations();
            double e_scf_t = 0.5 * (scf_.P_alpha.cwiseProduct(H_core_ + scf_.F_alpha).sum() +
                                    scf_.P_beta.cwiseProduct(H_core_ + scf_.F_beta).sum())
                             + mol_.nuclear_repulsion_energy();
            return e_scf_t + e_corr_t;
        };

        double E_plus = calc_tot(theta);
        double E_minus = calc_tot(-theta);
        double grad_num = (E_plus - E_minus) / (2.0 * theta);
        
        std::cout << "Gradien Numerik (FD) : " << std::scientific << grad_num << "\n";
        std::cout << "Gradien Analitik     : " << std::scientific << grad_ana << "\n";
        std::cout << "Selisih Absolut      : " << std::scientific << std::abs(grad_num - grad_ana) << "\n";
        std::cout << "---------------------------------------------------------\n";
                  
        C_a_current_ = C_a_orig;
        C_b_current_ = C_b_orig;
    }

    while (macro_iter < config_.max_iterations) {
        scf_.C_alpha = C_a_current_;
        scf_.C_beta  = C_b_current_;

        execute_micro_iterations();
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& F_ao_a = scf_.F_alpha;
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& F_ao_b = scf_.F_beta;

        double e_scf = 0.5 * (scf_.P_alpha.cwiseProduct(H_core_ + F_ao_a).sum() + 
                              scf_.P_beta.cwiseProduct(H_core_ + F_ao_b).sum()) 
                       + mol_.nuclear_repulsion_energy();

        double e_mp2_corr = get_correlation_energy(); 
        double e_tot = e_scf + e_mp2_corr;

        if (macro_iter > 0 && !step_rejected) {
            double actual_change = e_tot - e_total_last;
            double rho = actual_change / expected_change; 

            if (actual_change > 1e-7 || actual_change < -5.0 || std::isnan(e_tot) || std::isinf(e_tot)) {
                C_a_current_ = C_a_last; 
                C_b_current_ = C_b_last;
                trust_radius *= 0.25; 
                if (trust_radius <= 1e-5) {
                    if (omp_get_thread_num() == 0) std::cout << "  [" << method_tag << "] Trust radius minimum tercapai.\n";
                    is_converged = true;
                    break; 
                }
                scf_.P_alpha = C_a_current_.leftCols(na_) * C_a_current_.leftCols(na_).transpose();
                if (!is_restricted && nb_ > 0) scf_.P_beta = C_b_current_.leftCols(nb_) * C_b_current_.leftCols(nb_).transpose();
                else scf_.P_beta = scf_.P_alpha;
                
                expected_change = -1e-6; 
                step_rejected = true;
                continue; 
                
            } else {
                if (rho > 0.75) trust_radius = std::min(0.25, trust_radius * 1.5); 
                else if (rho < 0.25) trust_radius *= 0.5;
                
                if (trust_radius <= 1e-5) {
                    if (omp_get_thread_num() == 0) std::cout << "  [" << method_tag << "] Trust radius minimum tercapai.\n";
                    is_converged = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        step_rejected = false;

        e_total_best = e_tot;
        e_corr_best = e_mp2_corr;
        
        double delta_e = (macro_iter > 0) ? std::abs(e_tot - e_total_last) : 1e99;
        e_total_last = e_tot;
        C_a_last = C_a_current_; 
        C_b_last = C_b_current_;

        execute_macro_iterations(diis_alpha, diis_beta, macro_iter);
        double grad_norm = orbital_gradient_.norm();

        if (omp_get_thread_num() == 0) {
            std::cout << std::setw(4) << macro_iter << "    " 
                      << std::fixed << std::setprecision(8) << e_tot << "    "
                      << std::setprecision(8) << e_mp2_corr << "    " 
                      << std::scientific << std::setprecision(2) << grad_norm << "\n";
        }

        if (macro_iter > 0 && grad_norm < grad_thresh_ && delta_e < conv_thresh_) {
            if (!is_restricted && grad_norm > 1.0e-7) {
                Eigen::VectorXd polish_step = compute_soscf_step(trust_radius, expected_change);
                apply_orbital_rotation(polish_step);
                C_a_last = C_a_current_;
                C_b_last = C_b_current_;
            }
            is_converged = true;
            break;
        }

        Eigen::VectorXd actual_step = compute_soscf_step(trust_radius, expected_change);
        apply_orbital_rotation(actual_step);

        scf_.P_alpha = C_a_current_.leftCols(na_) * C_a_current_.leftCols(na_).transpose();
        if (!is_restricted && nb_ > 0) scf_.P_beta = C_b_current_.leftCols(nb_) * C_b_current_.leftCols(nb_).transpose();
        else scf_.P_beta = scf_.P_alpha;

        macro_iter++;
    }

    // Jika max_iterations <= 0, tetap jalankan 1 kali micro-iteration (pseudocanonicalize + evaluasi energi)
    if (config_.max_iterations <= 0) {
        scf_.C_alpha = C_a_current_;
        scf_.C_beta  = C_b_current_;
        execute_micro_iterations();
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& F_ao_a = scf_.F_alpha;
        const Eigen::MatrixXd& F_ao_b = scf_.F_beta;
        double e_scf = 0.5 * (scf_.P_alpha.cwiseProduct(H_core_ + F_ao_a).sum() + 
                              scf_.P_beta.cwiseProduct(H_core_ + F_ao_b).sum()) 
                       + mol_.nuclear_repulsion_energy();
        e_corr_best = get_correlation_energy();
        e_total_best = e_scf + e_corr_best;
        C_a_last = scf_.C_alpha;
        C_b_last = scf_.C_beta;
        is_converged = true;
    }

    MP2Result res;
    res.energy_total = e_total_best;
    res.energy_mp2_corr = e_corr_best;
    res.energy_mp2_ss = e_ss_; 
    res.energy_mp2_os = e_os_;
    res.energy_scf = e_total_best - e_corr_best; 
    res.converged = is_converged;
    res.iterations = macro_iter;
    res.C_alpha = C_a_last; 
    res.C_beta = C_b_last;
    res.orbital_energies_alpha = scf_.orbital_energies_alpha;
    res.orbital_energies_beta  = scf_.orbital_energies_beta;

    return res;
}

void OMP2::reset_diis() {}
Eigen::MatrixXd OMP2::build_opdm() { return G_oo_alpha_ + G_oo_beta_; } 
Eigen::MatrixXd OMP2::extrapolate_diis(std::vector<Eigen::MatrixXd>&, std::vector<Eigen::MatrixXd>&) { return Eigen::MatrixXd(); }

} // namespace mshqc

namespace tblis {

inline size_t view_num_elements(const varray_view<double>& v) {
    const auto& len = v.lengths();
    if (len.empty()) return 0;
    size_t prod = 1;
    for (auto d : len) {
        if (d <= 0) return 0;
        prod *= static_cast<size_t>(d);
    }
    return prod;
}

// Pengganti aman untuk tblis::mult<double> yang mencegah SIGSEGV pada TBLIS v1.2.0
// saat seluruh dimensi dari salah satu tensor bernilai 1 (misal O = 1 pada He, H2, Li-beta)
template <typename T = double>
inline void safe_tblis_mult(
    T alpha,
    const varray_view<double>& A, const char* idxA,
    const varray_view<double>& B, const char* idxB,
    T beta,
    varray_view<double>& C, const char* idxC)
{
    const size_t szA = view_num_elements(A);
    const size_t szB = view_num_elements(B);
    const size_t szC = view_num_elements(C);

    if (szC == 0) return;
    if (szA == 0 || szB == 0) {
        Eigen::Map<Eigen::VectorXd> vecC(C.data(), static_cast<Eigen::Index>(szC));
        if (beta == T(0)) vecC.setZero();
        else vecC *= static_cast<double>(beta);
        return;
    }

    // Kasus 1: Tensor output C berukuran 1 elemen (misal G_oo (1,1) atau G_oooo (1,1,1,1))
    if (szC == 1 && szA == szB) {
        Eigen::Map<const Eigen::VectorXd> vecA(A.data(), static_cast<Eigen::Index>(szA));
        Eigen::Map<const Eigen::VectorXd> vecB(B.data(), static_cast<Eigen::Index>(szB));
        const double dot = vecA.dot(vecB);
        C.data()[0] = (beta == T(0)) ? (alpha * dot) : (beta * C.data()[0] + alpha * dot);
        return;
    }
    // Kasus 2: Tensor input A berukuran 1 elemen (misal G_oooo (1,1,1,1) * V_ooov (1,1,1,V) -> Z (V,1))
    if (szA == 1 && szB == szC) {
        Eigen::Map<const Eigen::VectorXd> vecB(B.data(), static_cast<Eigen::Index>(szB));
        Eigen::Map<Eigen::VectorXd> vecC(C.data(), static_cast<Eigen::Index>(szC));
        const double scale = alpha * A.data()[0];
        if (beta == T(0)) vecC.noalias() = scale * vecB;
        else vecC.noalias() = beta * vecC + scale * vecB;
        return;
    }
    // Kasus 3: Tensor input B berukuran 1 elemen (misal T2 (1,1,V,V) * V_oooo (1,1,1,1) -> W (1,1,V,V))
    if (szB == 1 && szA == szC) {
        Eigen::Map<const Eigen::VectorXd> vecA(A.data(), static_cast<Eigen::Index>(szA));
        Eigen::Map<Eigen::VectorXd> vecC(C.data(), static_cast<Eigen::Index>(szC));
        const double scale = alpha * B.data()[0];
        if (beta == T(0)) vecC.noalias() = scale * vecA;
        else vecC.noalias() = beta * vecC + scale * vecA;
        return;
    }

    ::tblis::mult<double>(alpha, A, idxA, B, idxB, beta, C, idxC);
}

} // namespace tblis

#define mult safe_tblis_mult

namespace mshqc {

using integrals::ERITransformer;
using tblis::len_type;
using tblis::stride_type;
using tblis::varray_view;

BaseMP3::BaseMP3(const SCFResult& scf, const MP2Result& mp2, const MP2Config& config, std::shared_ptr<IntegralEngine> ints)
    : scf_(scf), mp2_(mp2), config_(config), ints_(ints) 
{
    // Normalisasi eri_method ke huruf kecil agar "EXACT", "CHOLESKY", "DF" selalu dikenali
    std::transform(config_.eri_method.begin(), config_.eri_method.end(), config_.eri_method.begin(),
                   [](unsigned char ch) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(ch)); });
    if (config_.eri_method == "df") {
        config_.use_df = true;
    } else if (!config_.use_df && config_.eri_method != "cholesky") {
        config_.eri_method = "exact";
    }

    nbf_ = scf_.C_alpha.rows();
    no_a_ = scf_.n_occ_alpha; no_b_ = scf_.n_occ_beta;
    nv_a_ = nbf_ - no_a_;     nv_b_ = nbf_ - no_b_;
    n_aux_ = scf_.L_mat.cols(); 
    
    if (mp2_.t2_aa.size() > 0) t2_aa_ = mp2_.t2_aa;
    if (mp2_.t2_bb.size() > 0) t2_bb_ = mp2_.t2_bb;
    if (mp2_.t2_ab.size() > 0) t2_ab_ = mp2_.t2_ab;
}

double BaseMP3::tensor_dot(const Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>& A, const Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>& B) const {
    Eigen::Map<const Eigen::VectorXd> vecA(A.data(), A.size());
    Eigen::Map<const Eigen::VectorXd> vecB(B.data(), B.size());
    return vecA.dot(vecB);
}

#define TBLIS_VIEW_4D(name, t, d1, d2, d3, d4) \
    varray_view<double> name({(len_type)d1, (len_type)d2, (len_type)d3, (len_type)d4}, t.data(), \
    {1, (stride_type)d1, (stride_type)(d1*d2), (stride_type)(d1*d2*d3)})

#define TBLIS_VIEW_3D(name, ptr, d1, d2, d3) \
    varray_view<double> name({(len_type)d1, (len_type)d2, (len_type)d3}, ptr, \
    {1, (stride_type)d1, (stride_type)(d1*d2)})

#define TBLIS_VIEW_2D(name, ptr, d1, d2) \
    varray_view<double> name({(len_type)d1, (len_type)d2}, ptr, \
    {1, (stride_type)d1})

MP3Result RMP3::compute() {
    auto t_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    if(omp_get_thread_num() == 0) std::cout << "\n=== RMP3 (Unified Native TBLIS - Cholesky) ===\n";

    const auto& Co = scf_.C_alpha.leftCols(no_a_);
    const auto& Cv = scf_.C_alpha.rightCols(nv_a_);

    TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_T, t2_aa_, no_a_, no_a_, nv_a_, nv_a_);
    Eigen::Tensor< double, 4 > W(no_a_, no_a_, nv_a_, nv_a_); W.setZero();
    TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_W, W, no_a_, no_a_, nv_a_, nv_a_);

    const Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>* eri_ao_ptr = nullptr;
    if (config_.eri_method == "exact") eri_ao_ptr = &(ints_->compute_eri());

    // Bypass TBLIS/MKL thread limits with Tiled Level-3 BLAS DGEMM & layout-respecting mapping
    auto make_V_from_B_inplace = [&](const Eigen::MatrixXd& B1, const Eigen::MatrixXd& B2, Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>& V_mo) {
        int d1 = B1.rows(); int d2 = B2.rows(); 
        if (d1 <= 0 || d2 <= 0 || B1.cols() <= 0 || B2.cols() <= 0) {
            V_mo.setZero();
            return false;
        }
        Eigen::MatrixXd V_mat(d1, d2);
        constexpr int tile = 64;
        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic)
        for (int i = 0; i < d1; i += tile) {
            int rows = std::min(tile, d1 - i);
            V_mat.middleRows(i, rows).noalias() = B1.middleRows(i, rows) * B2.transpose();
        }

        if (V_mat.cwiseAbs().maxCoeff() < 1e-12) {
            V_mo.setZero();
            return false;
        }
        
        int dim0 = V_mo.dimension(0);
        int dim1 = V_mo.dimension(1);
        int dim2 = V_mo.dimension(2);
        int dim3 = V_mo.dimension(3);

        #pragma omp parallel for collapse(4)
        for (int i = 0; i < dim0; ++i) {
            for (int j = 0; j < dim1; ++j) {
                for (int k = 0; k < dim2; ++k) {
                    for (int l = 0; l < dim3; ++l) {
                        int row = i * dim1 + j; 
                        int col = k * dim3 + l;
                        V_mo(i, j, k, l) = V_mat(row, col);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    };

    Eigen::MatrixXd B_oo, B_ov, B_vv;
    if (config_.eri_method != "exact") {
        B_ov = mp2_.B_ia_P_alpha; 
        
        auto build_B_fast = [&](const Eigen::MatrixXd& C1, const Eigen::MatrixXd& C2) {
            int d1 = C1.cols(); int d2 = C2.cols();
            Eigen::MatrixXd B = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(d1 * d2, n_aux_);
            Eigen::Map<const Eigen::MatrixXd> L_flat(scf_.L_mat.data(), nbf_, nbf_ * n_aux_);
            Eigen::MatrixXd temp1 = C1.transpose() * L_flat;
            #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
            for (int P = 0; P < n_aux_; ++P) {
                Eigen::Map<const Eigen::MatrixXd> tmp_P(temp1.data() + P * d1 * nbf_, d1, nbf_);
                Eigen::MatrixXd L_pq = tmp_P * C2;
                for (int x = 0; x < d1; ++x) {
                    for (int y = 0; y < d2; ++y) {
                        B(x * d2 + y, P) = L_pq(x, y);
                    }
                }
            }
            return B;
        };

        B_oo = build_B_fast(Co, Co);
        B_vv = build_B_fast(Cv, Cv);
    }

    // 1. Ladder term (vvvv) - AO-Driven Unique-Pair untuk Mode EXACT (0 MB V^4, identik dengan OMP3)
    if (config_.eri_method == "exact") {
        tblis::varray_view<double> t_eri_ao(
            {(len_type)nbf_, (len_type)nbf_, (len_type)nbf_, (len_type)nbf_},
            const_cast<double*>(eri_ao_ptr->data()),
            {1, (stride_type)nbf_, (stride_type)(nbf_*nbf_), (stride_type)(nbf_*nbf_*nbf_)}
        );
        int n_pairs_sym = no_a_ * (no_a_ + 1) / 2;
        Eigen::Tensor<double, 3> T_ao(n_pairs_sym, nbf_, nbf_);
        Eigen::Tensor<double, 3> W_ao(n_pairs_sym, nbf_, nbf_);
        W_ao.setZero();

        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
        for (int i = 0; i < no_a_; ++i) {
            for (int j = i; j < no_a_; ++j) {
                int k = i * no_a_ - (i * (i - 1)) / 2 + (j - i);
                Eigen::MatrixXd Tij_vv(nv_a_, nv_a_);
                for (int e = 0; e < nv_a_; ++e)
                    for (int f = 0; f < nv_a_; ++f)
                        Tij_vv(e, f) = t2_aa_(i, j, e, f);
                Eigen::MatrixXd Tij_ao = Cv * Tij_vv * Cv.transpose();
                for (int mu = 0; mu < nbf_; ++mu)
                    for (int lam = 0; lam < nbf_; ++lam)
                        T_ao(k, mu, lam) = Tij_ao(mu, lam);
            }
        }

        TBLIS_VIEW_3D(t_Tao, T_ao.data(), n_pairs_sym, nbf_, nbf_);
        TBLIS_VIEW_3D(t_Wao, W_ao.data(), n_pairs_sym, nbf_, nbf_);
        tblis::mult<double>(1.0, t_Tao, "kpr", t_eri_ao, "pqrs", 0.0, t_Wao, "kqs");

        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
        for (int i = 0; i < no_a_; ++i) {
            for (int j = i; j < no_a_; ++j) {
                int k = i * no_a_ - (i * (i - 1)) / 2 + (j - i);
                Eigen::MatrixXd Wij_ao(nbf_, nbf_);
                for (int nu = 0; nu < nbf_; ++nu)
                    for (int sig = 0; sig < nbf_; ++sig)
                        Wij_ao(nu, sig) = W_ao(k, nu, sig);
                Eigen::MatrixXd Wij_vv = Cv.transpose() * Wij_ao * Cv;
                for (int a = 0; a < nv_a_; ++a) {
                    for (int b = 0; b < nv_a_; ++b) {
                        W(i, j, a, b) += Wij_vv(a, b);
                        if (i != j) W(j, i, b, a) += Wij_vv(a, b);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        int n_pairs_sym = no_a_ * (no_a_ + 1) / 2;
        std::vector<Eigen::MatrixXd> T_pairs(n_pairs_sym, Eigen::MatrixXd(nv_a_, nv_a_));
        std::vector<Eigen::MatrixXd> W_pairs(n_pairs_sym, Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nv_a_, nv_a_));
        int p_idx = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < no_a_; ++i) {
            for (int j = i; j < no_a_; ++j) {
                for (int e = 0; e < nv_a_; ++e)
                    for (int f = 0; f < nv_a_; ++f)
                        T_pairs[p_idx](e, f) = t2_aa_(i, j, e, f);
                p_idx++;
            }
        }
        #pragma omp parallel
        {
            std::vector<Eigen::MatrixXd> W_loc(n_pairs_sym, Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nv_a_, nv_a_));
            Eigen::MatrixXd X_tmp(nv_a_, nv_a_);
            #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
            for (int P = 0; P < n_aux_; ++P) {
                Eigen::Map<const Eigen::MatrixXd> B_P(B_vv.col(P).data(), nv_a_, nv_a_);
                for (int k = 0; k < n_pairs_sym; ++k) {
                    X_tmp.noalias() = T_pairs[k] * B_P;
                    W_loc[k].noalias() += B_P.transpose() * X_tmp;
                }
            }
            #pragma omp critical
            {
                for (int k = 0; k < n_pairs_sym; ++k) W_pairs[k] += W_loc[k];
            }
        }
        p_idx = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < no_a_; ++i) {
            for (int j = i; j < no_a_; ++j) {
                for (int a = 0; a < nv_a_; ++a) {
                    for (int b = 0; b < nv_a_; ++b) {
                        W(i, j, a, b) += W_pairs[p_idx](a, b);
                        if (i != j) W(j, i, b, a) += W_pairs[p_idx](a, b);
                    }
                }
                p_idx++;
            }
        }
    }

    // 2 & 3. Ladder term (oooo) + Ring terms (ovov & oovv) dengan Shared Half-Bra (Co, Co)
    {
        Eigen::Tensor<double, 4> V(no_a_, no_a_, no_a_, no_a_);
        Eigen::Tensor<double, 4> V_ovov(no_a_, nv_a_, no_a_, nv_a_);
        Eigen::Tensor<double, 4> V_oovv(no_a_, no_a_, nv_a_, nv_a_);
        bool calc_ovov = true, calc_oovv = true;

        if (config_.eri_method == "exact") {
            auto half_oo = ERITransformer::transform_half_bra(*eri_ao_ptr, Co, Co, nbf_, no_a_, no_a_);
            V      = ERITransformer::transform_finish_ket(half_oo, Co, Co, nbf_, no_a_, no_a_, no_a_, no_a_);
            V_oovv = ERITransformer::transform_finish_ket(half_oo, Cv, Cv, nbf_, no_a_, no_a_, nv_a_, nv_a_);
            half_oo.resize(0, 0, 0, 0);
            V_ovov = ERITransformer::transform_custom(*eri_ao_ptr, Co, Cv, Co, Cv, nbf_, no_a_, nv_a_, no_a_, nv_a_);

            TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_V, V, no_a_, no_a_, no_a_, no_a_);
            tblis::mult< double >(1.0, t_T, "mnab", t_V, "minj", 1.0, t_W, "ijab");
        } else {
            if (make_V_from_B_inplace(B_oo, B_oo, V)) {
                TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_V, V, no_a_, no_a_, no_a_, no_a_);
                tblis::mult< double >(1.0, t_T, "mnab", t_V, "minj", 1.0, t_W, "ijab");
            }
            calc_ovov = make_V_from_B_inplace(B_ov, B_ov, V_ovov);
            calc_oovv = make_V_from_B_inplace(B_oo, B_vv, V_oovv);
        }

        TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_Vovov, V_ovov, no_a_, nv_a_, no_a_, nv_a_);
        TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_Voovv, V_oovv, no_a_, no_a_, nv_a_, nv_a_);

        if (calc_ovov) {
            tblis::mult< double >(2.0,  t_Vovov, "iakc", t_T, "kjcb", 1.0, t_W, "ijab");
            tblis::mult< double >(2.0,  t_T, "ikac", t_Vovov, "kcjb", 1.0, t_W, "ijab");
            tblis::mult< double >(-1.0, t_T, "ikca", t_Vovov, "kcjb", 1.0, t_W, "ijab");
            tblis::mult< double >(-1.0, t_Vovov, "iakc", t_T, "kjbc", 1.0, t_W, "ijab");
        }
        if (calc_oovv) {
            tblis::mult< double >(-1.0, t_Voovv, "ikac", t_T, "kjcb", 1.0, t_W, "ijab");
            tblis::mult< double >(-1.0, t_T, "ikac", t_Voovv, "kjcb", 1.0, t_W, "ijab");
            tblis::mult< double >(-1.0, t_Voovv, "ikbc", t_T, "kjac", 1.0, t_W, "ijab");
            tblis::mult< double >(-1.0, t_T, "ikcb", t_Voovv, "jkac", 1.0, t_W, "ijab");
        }
    }

    double e_mp3 = 0.0;
    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(4) reduction(+:e_mp3)
    for (int i = 0; i < no_a_; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j < no_a_; ++j) {
            for (int a = 0; a < nv_a_; ++a) {
                for (int b = 0; b < nv_a_; ++b) {
                    e_mp3 += W(i, j, a, b) * (2.0 * t2_aa_(i, j, a, b) - t2_aa_(i, j, b, a));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    MP3Result res;
    res.e_hf = scf_.energy_total;
    res.e_mp2 = mp2_.energy_mp2_corr;
    res.e3_aa = 0.0; res.e3_ab = 0.0; res.e_mp3 = e_mp3;
    res.e_corr_total = res.e_mp2 + res.e_mp3;
    res.e_total = res.e_hf + res.e_corr_total;

    auto t_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    if(omp_get_thread_num() == 0) {
        std::cout << "  E_MP3        : " << std::fixed << std::setprecision(8) << res.e_mp3 << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "  Total Energy : " << res.e_total << " Ha\n";
        std::cout << "  Time         : " << std::chrono::duration< double >(t_end - t_start).count() << " s\n";
    }
    return res;
}

MP3Result UMP3::compute() {
    auto t_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    if(omp_get_thread_num() == 0) std::cout << "\n=== UMP3 (Unified Native TBLIS - Cholesky) ===\n";

    const auto& Cao = scf_.C_alpha.leftCols(no_a_); const auto& Cav = scf_.C_alpha.rightCols(nv_a_);
    const auto& Cbo = scf_.C_beta.leftCols(no_b_);  const auto& Cbv = scf_.C_beta.rightCols(nv_b_);
    double e3_aa = 0.0, e3_bb = 0.0, e3_ab = 0.0;

    Eigen::Tensor< double, 4 > dummy_bb, dummy_ab;
    double* ptr_bb = nullptr;
    double* ptr_ab = nullptr;

    if (no_b_ > 0 && nv_b_ > 0) {
        if (t2_bb_.size() == 0) {
            dummy_bb = Eigen::Tensor< double, 4 >(no_b_, no_b_, nv_b_, nv_b_);
            dummy_bb.setZero();
            ptr_bb = dummy_bb.data();
        } else {
            ptr_bb = t2_bb_.data();
        }

        if (t2_ab_.size() == 0) {
            dummy_ab = Eigen::Tensor< double, 4 >(no_a_, no_b_, nv_a_, nv_b_);
            dummy_ab.setZero();
            ptr_ab = dummy_ab.data();
        } else {
            ptr_ab = t2_ab_.data();
        }
    }
    
    TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_Taa, t2_aa_, no_a_, no_a_, nv_a_, nv_a_);
    varray_view< double > t_Tbb({(len_type)no_b_, (len_type)no_b_, (len_type)nv_b_, (len_type)nv_b_}, 
        ptr_bb, {1, (stride_type)no_b_, (stride_type)(no_b_*no_b_), (stride_type)(no_b_*no_b_*nv_b_)});
    varray_view< double > t_Tab({(len_type)no_a_, (len_type)no_b_, (len_type)nv_a_, (len_type)nv_b_}, 
        ptr_ab, {1, (stride_type)no_a_, (stride_type)(no_a_*no_b_), (stride_type)(no_a_*no_b_*nv_a_)});

    Eigen::Tensor< double, 4 > Waa(no_a_, no_a_, nv_a_, nv_a_); TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_Waa, Waa, no_a_, no_a_, nv_a_, nv_a_);
    Eigen::Tensor< double, 4 > Wbb(no_b_, no_b_, nv_b_, nv_b_); TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_Wbb, Wbb, no_b_, no_b_, nv_b_, nv_b_);
    Eigen::Tensor< double, 4 > Wab(no_a_, no_b_, nv_a_, nv_b_); TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_Wab, Wab, no_a_, no_b_, nv_a_, nv_b_);

    const Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>* eri_ao_ptr = nullptr;
    if (config_.eri_method == "exact") eri_ao_ptr = &(ints_->compute_eri());

    auto make_V_from_B_inplace = [&](const Eigen::MatrixXd& B1, const Eigen::MatrixXd& B2, Eigen::Tensor<double, 4>& V_mo) {
        int d1 = B1.rows(); int d2 = B2.rows(); 
        if (d1 <= 0 || d2 <= 0 || B1.cols() <= 0 || B2.cols() <= 0) {
            V_mo.setZero();
            return false;
        }
        Eigen::MatrixXd V_mat(d1, d2);
        constexpr int tile = 64;
        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic)
        for (int i = 0; i < d1; i += tile) {
            int rows = std::min(tile, d1 - i);
            V_mat.middleRows(i, rows).noalias() = B1.middleRows(i, rows) * B2.transpose();
        }

        if (V_mat.cwiseAbs().maxCoeff() < 1e-12) {
            V_mo.setZero();
            return false;
        }

        int dim0 = V_mo.dimension(0);
        int dim1 = V_mo.dimension(1);
        int dim2 = V_mo.dimension(2);
        int dim3 = V_mo.dimension(3);

        #pragma omp parallel for collapse(4)
        for (int i = 0; i < dim0; ++i) {
            for (int j = 0; j < dim1; ++j) {
                for (int k = 0; k < dim2; ++k) {
                    for (int l = 0; l < dim3; ++l) {
                        int row = i * dim1 + j; 
                        int col = k * dim3 + l;
                        V_mo(i, j, k, l) = V_mat(row, col);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    };

    Eigen::MatrixXd B_oo_a, B_ov_a, B_vv_a;
    Eigen::MatrixXd B_oo_b, B_ov_b, B_vv_b;
    if (config_.eri_method != "exact") {
        B_ov_a = mp2_.B_ia_P_alpha; 
        if (no_b_ > 0 && nv_b_ > 0) B_ov_b = mp2_.B_ia_P_beta;

        auto build_B_fast = [&](const Eigen::MatrixXd& C1, const Eigen::MatrixXd& C2) {
            int d1 = C1.cols(); int d2 = C2.cols();
            Eigen::MatrixXd B = Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(d1 * d2, n_aux_);
            Eigen::Map<const Eigen::MatrixXd> L_flat(scf_.L_mat.data(), nbf_, nbf_ * n_aux_);
            Eigen::MatrixXd temp1 = C1.transpose() * L_flat;
            #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
            for (int P = 0; P < n_aux_; ++P) {
                Eigen::Map<const Eigen::MatrixXd> tmp_P(temp1.data() + P * d1 * nbf_, d1, nbf_);
                Eigen::MatrixXd L_pq = tmp_P * C2;
                for (int x = 0; x < d1; ++x) {
                    for (int y = 0; y < d2; ++y) {
                        B(x * d2 + y, P) = L_pq(x, y);
                    }
                }
            }
            return B;
        };

        B_oo_a = build_B_fast(Cao, Cao);
        B_vv_a = build_B_fast(Cav, Cav);

        if (no_b_ > 0 && nv_b_ > 0) {
            B_oo_b = build_B_fast(Cbo, Cbo);
            B_vv_b = build_B_fast(Cbv, Cbv);
        }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 1. TAHAP LADDER (vvvv) - Batched Unique-Pair AO-Driven untuk Mode EXACT
    // =========================================================================
    if (config_.eri_method == "exact") {
        tblis::varray_view<double> t_eri_ao(
            {(len_type)nbf_, (len_type)nbf_, (len_type)nbf_, (len_type)nbf_},
            const_cast<double*>(eri_ao_ptr->data()),
            {1, (stride_type)nbf_, (stride_type)(nbf_*nbf_), (stride_type)(nbf_*nbf_*nbf_)}
        );
        const bool has_beta = (no_b_ > 0 && nv_b_ > 0);
        const int n_pairs_aa = no_a_ * (no_a_ - 1) / 2;
        const int n_pairs_bb = has_beta ? (no_b_ * (no_b_ - 1) / 2) : 0;
        const int n_pairs_ab = has_beta ? (no_a_ * no_b_) : 0;
        const int n_tot_pairs = n_pairs_aa + n_pairs_bb + n_pairs_ab;

        if (n_tot_pairs > 0) {
            Eigen::Tensor<double, 3> T_ao_batch(n_tot_pairs, nbf_, nbf_);
            Eigen::Tensor<double, 3> W_ao_batch(n_tot_pairs, nbf_, nbf_);
            W_ao_batch.setZero();

            int p_idx = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < no_a_; ++i) {
                for (int j = i + 1; j < no_a_; ++j) {
                    Eigen::MatrixXd Tij_vv(nv_a_, nv_a_);
                    for (int e = 0; e < nv_a_; ++e)
                        for (int f = 0; f < nv_a_; ++f)
                            Tij_vv(e, f) = t2_aa_(i, j, e, f);
                    Eigen::MatrixXd Tij_ao = Cav * Tij_vv * Cav.transpose();
                    for (int mu = 0; mu < nbf_; ++mu)
                        for (int lam = 0; lam < nbf_; ++lam)
                            T_ao_batch(p_idx, mu, lam) = Tij_ao(mu, lam);
                    p_idx++;
                }
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < no_b_; ++i) {
                for (int j = i + 1; j < no_b_; ++j) {
                    Eigen::MatrixXd Tij_vv(nv_b_, nv_b_);
                    for (int e = 0; e < nv_b_; ++e)
                        for (int f = 0; f < nv_b_; ++f)
                            Tij_vv(e, f) = t2_bb_(i, j, e, f);
                    Eigen::MatrixXd Tij_ao = Cbv * Tij_vv * Cbv.transpose();
                    for (int mu = 0; mu < nbf_; ++mu)
                        for (int lam = 0; lam < nbf_; ++lam)
                            T_ao_batch(p_idx, mu, lam) = Tij_ao(mu, lam);
                    p_idx++;
                }
            }
            if (has_beta) {
                for (int i = 0; i < no_a_; ++i) {
                    for (int j = 0; j < no_b_; ++j) {
                        Eigen::MatrixXd Tij_vv(nv_a_, nv_b_);
                        for (int e = 0; e < nv_a_; ++e)
                            for (int f = 0; f < nv_b_; ++f)
                                Tij_vv(e, f) = t2_ab_(i, j, e, f);
                        Eigen::MatrixXd Tij_ao = Cav * Tij_vv * Cbv.transpose();
                        for (int mu = 0; mu < nbf_; ++mu)
                            for (int lam = 0; lam < nbf_; ++lam)
                                T_ao_batch(p_idx, mu, lam) = Tij_ao(mu, lam);
                        p_idx++;
                    }
                }
            }

            TBLIS_VIEW_3D(t_Tao_all, T_ao_batch.data(), n_tot_pairs, nbf_, nbf_);
            TBLIS_VIEW_3D(t_Wao_all, W_ao_batch.data(), n_tot_pairs, nbf_, nbf_);
            tblis::mult<double>(1.0, t_Tao_all, "kpr", t_eri_ao, "pqrs", 0.0, t_Wao_all, "kqs");

            p_idx = 0;
            double sum_aa = 0.0, sum_bb = 0.0, sum_ab = 0.0;
            for (int i = 0; i < no_a_; ++i) {
                for (int j = i + 1; j < no_a_; ++j) {
                    Eigen::MatrixXd Wij_ao(nbf_, nbf_);
                    for (int nu = 0; nu < nbf_; ++nu)
                        for (int sig = 0; sig < nbf_; ++sig)
                            Wij_ao(nu, sig) = W_ao_batch(p_idx, nu, sig);
                    Eigen::MatrixXd M_vv = Cav.transpose() * Wij_ao * Cav;
                    for (int a = 0; a < nv_a_; ++a)
                        for (int b = 0; b < nv_a_; ++b)
                            sum_aa += 2.0 * t2_aa_(i, j, a, b) * (M_vv(a, b) - M_vv(b, a));
                    p_idx++;
                }
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < no_b_; ++i) {
                for (int j = i + 1; j < no_b_; ++j) {
                    Eigen::MatrixXd Wij_ao(nbf_, nbf_);
                    for (int nu = 0; nu < nbf_; ++nu)
                        for (int sig = 0; sig < nbf_; ++sig)
                            Wij_ao(nu, sig) = W_ao_batch(p_idx, nu, sig);
                    Eigen::MatrixXd M_vv = Cbv.transpose() * Wij_ao * Cbv;
                    for (int a = 0; a < nv_b_; ++a)
                        for (int b = 0; b < nv_b_; ++b)
                            sum_bb += 2.0 * t2_bb_(i, j, a, b) * (M_vv(a, b) - M_vv(b, a));
                    p_idx++;
                }
            }
            if (has_beta) {
                for (int i = 0; i < no_a_; ++i) {
                    for (int j = 0; j < no_b_; ++j) {
                        Eigen::MatrixXd Wij_ao(nbf_, nbf_);
                        for (int nu = 0; nu < nbf_; ++nu)
                            for (int sig = 0; sig < nbf_; ++sig)
                                Wij_ao(nu, sig) = W_ao_batch(p_idx, nu, sig);
                        Eigen::MatrixXd M_vv = Cav.transpose() * Wij_ao * Cbv;
                        for (int a = 0; a < nv_a_; ++a)
                            for (int b = 0; b < nv_b_; ++b)
                                sum_ab += t2_ab_(i, j, a, b) * M_vv(a, b);
                        p_idx++;
                    }
                }
            }
            e3_aa += 0.125 * sum_aa;
            e3_bb += 0.125 * sum_bb;
            e3_ab += sum_ab;
        }
    } else {
        int n_pairs_aa = no_a_ * (no_a_ - 1) / 2;
        if (n_pairs_aa > 0) {
            std::vector<Eigen::MatrixXd> T_aa_pairs(n_pairs_aa, Eigen::MatrixXd(nv_a_, nv_a_));
            std::vector<Eigen::MatrixXd> W_aa_pairs(n_pairs_aa, Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nv_a_, nv_a_));
            int p_idx = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < no_a_; ++i) {
                for (int j = i + 1; j < no_a_; ++j) {
                    for (int e = 0; e < nv_a_; ++e)
                        for (int f = 0; f < nv_a_; ++f)
                            T_aa_pairs[p_idx](e, f) = t2_aa_(i, j, e, f);
                    p_idx++;
                }
            }
            #pragma omp parallel
            {
                std::vector<Eigen::MatrixXd> W_loc(n_pairs_aa, Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nv_a_, nv_a_));
                Eigen::MatrixXd X_tmp(nv_a_, nv_a_);
                #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
                for (int P = 0; P < n_aux_; ++P) {
                    Eigen::Map<const Eigen::MatrixXd> B_P(B_vv_a.col(P).data(), nv_a_, nv_a_);
                    for (int k = 0; k < n_pairs_aa; ++k) {
                        X_tmp.noalias() = T_aa_pairs[k] * B_P;
                        W_loc[k].noalias() += B_P.transpose() * X_tmp;
                    }
                }
                #pragma omp critical
                {
                    for (int k = 0; k < n_pairs_aa; ++k) W_aa_pairs[k] += W_loc[k];
                }
            }
            double sum_aa = 0.0;
            for (int k = 0; k < n_pairs_aa; ++k) {
                Eigen::MatrixXd W_asym = W_aa_pairs[k] - W_aa_pairs[k].transpose();
                sum_aa += 2.0 * (T_aa_pairs[k].cwiseProduct(W_asym)).sum();
            }
            e3_aa += 0.125 * sum_aa;
        }
        if (no_b_ > 0 && nv_b_ > 0) {
            int n_pairs_bb = no_b_ * (no_b_ - 1) / 2;
            if (n_pairs_bb > 0) {
                std::vector<Eigen::MatrixXd> T_bb_pairs(n_pairs_bb, Eigen::MatrixXd(nv_b_, nv_b_));
                std::vector<Eigen::MatrixXd> W_bb_pairs(n_pairs_bb, Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nv_b_, nv_b_));
                int p_idx = 0;
                for (int i = 0; i < no_b_; ++i) {
                    for (int j = i + 1; j < no_b_; ++j) {
                        for (int e = 0; e < nv_b_; ++e)
                            for (int f = 0; f < nv_b_; ++f)
                                T_bb_pairs[p_idx](e, f) = t2_bb_(i, j, e, f);
                        p_idx++;
                    }
                }
                #pragma omp parallel
                {
                    std::vector<Eigen::MatrixXd> W_loc(n_pairs_bb, Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nv_b_, nv_b_));
                    Eigen::MatrixXd X_tmp(nv_b_, nv_b_);
                    #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
                    for (int P = 0; P < n_aux_; ++P) {
                        Eigen::Map<const Eigen::MatrixXd> B_P(B_vv_b.col(P).data(), nv_b_, nv_b_);
                        for (int k = 0; k < n_pairs_bb; ++k) {
                            X_tmp.noalias() = T_bb_pairs[k] * B_P;
                            W_loc[k].noalias() += B_P.transpose() * X_tmp;
                        }
                    }
                    #pragma omp critical
                    {
                        for (int k = 0; k < n_pairs_bb; ++k) W_bb_pairs[k] += W_loc[k];
                    }
                }
                double sum_bb = 0.0;
                for (int k = 0; k < n_pairs_bb; ++k) {
                    Eigen::MatrixXd W_asym = W_bb_pairs[k] - W_bb_pairs[k].transpose();
                    sum_bb += 2.0 * (T_bb_pairs[k].cwiseProduct(W_asym)).sum();
                }
                e3_bb += 0.125 * sum_bb;
            }
            int n_pairs_ab = no_a_ * no_b_;
            std::vector<Eigen::MatrixXd> T_ab_pairs(n_pairs_ab, Eigen::MatrixXd(nv_a_, nv_b_));
            std::vector<Eigen::MatrixXd> W_ab_pairs(n_pairs_ab, Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nv_a_, nv_b_));
            for (int i = 0; i < no_a_; ++i) {
                for (int j = 0; j < no_b_; ++j) {
                    int k = i * no_b_ + j;
                    for (int e = 0; e < nv_a_; ++e)
                        for (int f = 0; f < nv_b_; ++f)
                            T_ab_pairs[k](e, f) = t2_ab_(i, j, e, f);
                }
            }
            #pragma omp parallel
            {
                std::vector<Eigen::MatrixXd> W_loc(n_pairs_ab, Eigen::MatrixXd::Zero(nv_a_, nv_b_));
                Eigen::MatrixXd X_tmp(nv_a_, nv_b_);
                #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
                for (int P = 0; P < n_aux_; ++P) {
                    Eigen::Map<const Eigen::MatrixXd> B_Pa(B_vv_a.col(P).data(), nv_a_, nv_a_);
                    Eigen::Map<const Eigen::MatrixXd> B_Pb(B_vv_b.col(P).data(), nv_b_, nv_b_);
                    for (int k = 0; k < n_pairs_ab; ++k) {
                        X_tmp.noalias() = T_ab_pairs[k] * B_Pb;
                        W_loc[k].noalias() += B_Pa.transpose() * X_tmp;
                    }
                }
                #pragma omp critical
                {
                    for (int k = 0; k < n_pairs_ab; ++k) W_ab_pairs[k] += W_loc[k];
                }
            }
            double sum_ab = 0.0;
            for (int k = 0; k < n_pairs_ab; ++k) {
                sum_ab += (T_ab_pairs[k].cwiseProduct(W_ab_pairs[k])).sum();
            }
            e3_ab += sum_ab;
        }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 2 & 3. TAHAP LADDER (oooo) & RING (ovov & oovv) DENGAN SHARED HALF-BRA
    // =========================================================================
    Eigen::Tensor<double, 4> half_oo_a, half_oo_b, half_ov_a;
    if (config_.eri_method == "exact") {
        half_oo_a = ERITransformer::transform_half_bra(*eri_ao_ptr, Cao, Cao, nbf_, no_a_, no_a_);
        half_ov_a = ERITransformer::transform_half_bra(*eri_ao_ptr, Cao, Cav, nbf_, no_a_, nv_a_);
        if (no_b_ > 0 && nv_b_ > 0) {
            half_oo_b = ERITransformer::transform_half_bra(*eri_ao_ptr, Cbo, Cbo, nbf_, no_b_, no_b_);
        }
    }

    // --- 2. LADDER (oooo) ---
    {
        Eigen::Tensor<double, 4> Vaa(no_a_, no_a_, no_a_, no_a_);
        if (config_.eri_method == "exact") {
            if (no_a_ > 1) {
                Vaa = ERITransformer::transform_finish_ket(half_oo_a, Cao, Cao, nbf_, no_a_, no_a_, no_a_, no_a_);
                TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_Vaa, Vaa, no_a_, no_a_, no_a_, no_a_); Waa.setZero();
                tblis::mult< double >(1.0, t_Taa, "mnab", t_Vaa, "minj", 1.0, t_Waa, "ijab");
                tblis::mult< double >(-1.0, t_Taa, "mnab", t_Vaa, "mjni", 1.0, t_Waa, "ijab");
                e3_aa += 0.125 * tensor_dot(t2_aa_, Waa);
            }
        } else {
            if (no_a_ > 1 && make_V_from_B_inplace(B_oo_a, B_oo_a, Vaa)) {
                TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_Vaa, Vaa, no_a_, no_a_, no_a_, no_a_); Waa.setZero();
                tblis::mult< double >(1.0, t_Taa, "mnab", t_Vaa, "minj", 1.0, t_Waa, "ijab");
                tblis::mult< double >(-1.0, t_Taa, "mnab", t_Vaa, "mjni", 1.0, t_Waa, "ijab");
                e3_aa += 0.125 * tensor_dot(t2_aa_, Waa);
            }
        }

        if (no_b_ > 0 && nv_b_ > 0) {
            Eigen::Tensor<double, 4> Vbb(no_b_, no_b_, no_b_, no_b_);
            if (config_.eri_method == "exact") {
                if (no_b_ > 1) {
                    Vbb = ERITransformer::transform_finish_ket(half_oo_b, Cbo, Cbo, nbf_, no_b_, no_b_, no_b_, no_b_);
                    TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_Vbb, Vbb, no_b_, no_b_, no_b_, no_b_); Wbb.setZero();
                    tblis::mult< double >(1.0, t_Tbb, "mnab", t_Vbb, "minj", 1.0, t_Wbb, "ijab");
                    tblis::mult< double >(-1.0, t_Tbb, "mnab", t_Vbb, "mjni", 1.0, t_Wbb, "ijab");
                    e3_bb += 0.125 * tensor_dot(t2_bb_, Wbb);
                }
            } else {
                if (no_b_ > 1 && make_V_from_B_inplace(B_oo_b, B_oo_b, Vbb)) {
                    TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_Vbb, Vbb, no_b_, no_b_, no_b_, no_b_); Wbb.setZero();
                    tblis::mult< double >(1.0, t_Tbb, "mnab", t_Vbb, "minj", 1.0, t_Wbb, "ijab");
                    tblis::mult< double >(-1.0, t_Tbb, "mnab", t_Vbb, "mjni", 1.0, t_Wbb, "ijab");
                    e3_bb += 0.125 * tensor_dot(t2_bb_, Wbb);
                }
            }

            Eigen::Tensor<double, 4> Vab(no_a_, no_a_, no_b_, no_b_);
            if (config_.eri_method == "exact") {
                Vab = ERITransformer::transform_finish_ket(half_oo_a, Cbo, Cbo, nbf_, no_a_, no_a_, no_b_, no_b_);
                TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_Vab, Vab, no_a_, no_a_, no_b_, no_b_); Wab.setZero();
                tblis::mult< double >(1.0, t_Tab, "mnab", t_Vab, "minj", 1.0, t_Wab, "ijab");
                e3_ab += 1.0 * tensor_dot(t2_ab_, Wab);
            } else {
                if (make_V_from_B_inplace(B_oo_a, B_oo_b, Vab)) {
                    TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_Vab, Vab, no_a_, no_a_, no_b_, no_b_); Wab.setZero();
                    tblis::mult< double >(1.0, t_Tab, "mnab", t_Vab, "minj", 1.0, t_Wab, "ijab");
                    e3_ab += 1.0 * tensor_dot(t2_ab_, Wab);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // --- 3. RING (ovov & oovv) ---
    {
        Eigen::Tensor<double, 4> ovov_aa(no_a_, nv_a_, no_a_, nv_a_); 
        Eigen::Tensor<double, 4> oovv_aa(no_a_, no_a_, nv_a_, nv_a_); 
        bool calc_aa = true;

        if (config_.eri_method == "exact") {
            ovov_aa = ERITransformer::transform_finish_ket(half_ov_a, Cao, Cav, nbf_, no_a_, nv_a_, no_a_, nv_a_);
            oovv_aa = ERITransformer::transform_finish_ket(half_oo_a, Cav, Cav, nbf_, no_a_, no_a_, nv_a_, nv_a_);
        } else {
            calc_aa = make_V_from_B_inplace(B_ov_a, B_ov_a, ovov_aa);
            calc_aa &= make_V_from_B_inplace(B_oo_a, B_vv_a, oovv_aa);
        }

        TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_ovov_aa, ovov_aa, no_a_, nv_a_, no_a_, nv_a_);
        TBLIS_VIEW_4D(t_oovv_aa, oovv_aa, no_a_, no_a_, nv_a_, nv_a_);

        Waa.setZero();
        if (calc_aa && no_a_ > 1) {
            tblis::mult< double >(1.0, t_ovov_aa, "iakc", t_Taa, "kjcb", 1.0, t_Waa, "ijab");
            tblis::mult< double >(-1.0, t_oovv_aa, "ikca", t_Taa, "kjcb", 1.0, t_Waa, "ijab");
        }
